ब्रह्मणश्च ते पादं ब्रवाणीति ब्रवीतु मे भगवानिति तस्मै होवाच प्राची दिक्कला प्रतीची दिक्कला दक्षिणा दिक्कलोदीची दिक्कलैष वै सोम्य चतुष्कलः पादो ब्रह्मणः प्रकाशवान्नाम ॥ ४.५.२ ॥

*brahmaṇaśca te pādaṃ bravāṇīti bravītu me bhagavāniti tasmai hovāca prācī dikkalā pratīcī dikkalā dakṣiṇā dikkalodīcī dikkalaiṣa vai somya catuṣkalaḥ pādo brahmaṇaḥ prakāśavānnāma || 4.5.2 ||*

The bull said, 'I will also tell you about one foot of Brahman.' Satyakāma answered, 'Yes, lord, please do.' The bull told him: 'East is one part of Brahman, west is a second, south is a third, and north is a fourth. O Somya, this is one foot of Brahman, made up of four parts. Its name is Prakāśavān, the Shining.'

### 4.5.3

स य एतमेवं विद्वांश्चतुष्कलं पादं ब्रह्मणः प्रकाशवानित्युपास्ते प्रकाशवानस्मिँल्लोके भवति प्रकाशवतो ह लोकाञ्जयति य एतमेवं विद्वांश्चतुष्कलं पादं ब्रह्मणः प्रकाशवानित्युपास्ते ॥ ४.५.३ ॥ ॥ इति पञ्चमः खण्डः ॥

*sa ya etamevaṃ vidvāṃścatuṣkalaṃ pādaṃ brahmaṇaḥ prakāśavānityupāste prakāśavānasmim̐lloke bhavati prakāśavato ha lokāñjayati ya etamevaṃ vidvāṃścatuṣkalaṃ pādaṃ brahmaṇaḥ prakāśavānityupāste || 4.5.3 || || iti pañcamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

'Whoever knows this four-part foot of Brahman called "the Shining" and worships it as that wins fame in this world. Whoever knows this four-part foot of Brahman called "the Shining" and worships it as that reaches other worlds full of light.'

### 4.6.1

अग्निष्टे पादं वक्तेति स ह श्वोभूते ग आभिप्रस्थापयांचकार ता यत्राभि सायं बभूवुस्तत्राग्निमुपसमाधाय गा उपरुध्य समिधमाधाय पश्चादग्नेः प्राङुपोपविवेश ॥ ४.६.१ ॥

*agniṣṭe pādaṃ vakteti sa ha śvobhūte ga ābhiprasthāpayāṃcakāra tā yatrābhi sāyaṃ babhūvustatrāgnimupasamādhāya gā uparudhya samidhamādhāya paścādagneḥ prāṅupopaviveśa || 4.6.1 ||*

The bull then said, 'Agni (fire) will tell you about another foot of Brahman.' The following day Satyakāma gathered the cows and drove them on toward his teacher's house. At dusk they reached a spot where they stopped for the night. He penned the cows, gathered some fuel, lit a fire, and sat down just behind it, facing east.

### 4.6.2

तमग्निरभ्युवाद सत्यकाम३ इति भगव इति ह प्रतिशुश्राव ॥ ४.६.२ ॥

*tamagnirabhyuvāda satyakāma3 iti bhagava iti ha pratiśuśrāva || 4.6.2 ||*

The fire called out to him, 'O Satyakāma.' He answered, 'Yes, lord.'

### 4.6.3

ब्रह्मणः सोम्य ते पादं ब्रवाणीति ब्रवीतु मे भगवानिति तस्मै होवाच पृथिवी कलान्तरिक्षं कला द्यौः कला समुद्रः कलैष वै सोम्य चतुष्कलः पादो ब्रह्मणोऽनन्तवान्नाम ॥ ४.६.३ ॥

*brahmaṇaḥ somya te pādaṃ bravāṇīti bravītu me bhagavāniti tasmai hovāca pṛthivī kalāntarikṣaṃ kalā dyauḥ kalā samudraḥ kalaiṣa vai somya catuṣkalaḥ pādo brahmaṇo'nantavānnāma || 4.6.3 ||*

The fire said, 'O Somya, I will tell you about one foot of Brahman.' Satyakāma answered, 'Yes, lord, please do.' The fire told him: 'Earth is one part, the mid-region is a second, heaven is a third, and the ocean is a fourth. O Somya, these four parts make up a foot of Brahman. Its name is Anantavān, the Unlimited.'

### 4.6.4

स य एतमेवं विद्वांश्चतुष्कलं पादं ब्रह्मणोऽनन्तवानित्युपास्तेऽनन्तवानस्मिँल्लोके भवत्यनन्तवतो ह लोकाञ्जयति य एतमेवं विद्वांश्चतुष्कलं पादं ब्रह्मणोऽनन्तवानित्युपास्ते ॥ ४.६.४ ॥ ॥ इति षष्ठः खण्डः ॥

*sa ya etamevaṃ vidvāṃścatuṣkalaṃ pādaṃ brahmaṇo'nantavānityupāste'nantavānasmim̐lloke bhavatyanantavato ha lokāñjayati ya etamevaṃ vidvāṃścatuṣkalaṃ pādaṃ brahmaṇo'nantavānityupāste || 4.6.4 || || iti ṣaṣṭhaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

'Whoever knows this four-part foot of Brahman called "the Unlimited" and worships it as that enjoys long life in this world. Whoever knows this four-part foot of Brahman called "the Unlimited" and worships it as that reaches worlds that long endure.'

### 4.7.1

हंसस्ते पादं वक्तेति स ह श्वोभूते गा अभिप्रस्थापयांचकार ता यत्राभि सायं बभूवुस्तत्राग्निमुपसमाधाय गा उपरुध्य समिधमाधाय पश्चादग्नेः प्राङुपोपविवेश ॥ ४.७.१ ॥

*haṃsaste pādaṃ vakteti sa ha śvobhūte gā abhiprasthāpayāṃcakāra tā yatrābhi sāyaṃ babhūvustatrāgnimupasamādhāya gā uparudhya samidhamādhāya paścādagneḥ prāṅupopaviveśa || 4.7.1 ||*

The fire then said, 'The swan will tell you about another foot of Brahman.' The following day Satyakāma gathered the cows and drove them on toward his teacher's house. At dusk they reached a spot where they stopped for the night. He penned the cows, gathered some fuel, lit a fire, and sat down just behind it, facing east.

### 4.7.2

तंहंस उपनिपत्याभ्युवाद सत्यकाम३ इति भगव इति ह प्रतिशुश्राव ॥ ४.७.२ ॥

*taṃhaṃsa upanipatyābhyuvāda satyakāma3 iti bhagava iti ha pratiśuśrāva || 4.7.2 ||*

A swan flew down to him and said, 'O Satyakāma.' He answered, 'Yes, lord.'

### 4.7.3

ब्रह्मणः सोम्य ते पादं ब्रवाणीति ब्रवीतु मे भगवानिति तस्मै होवाचाग्निः कला सूर्यः कला चन्द्रः कला विद्युत्कलैष वै सोम्य चतुष्कलः पादो ब्रह्मणो ज्योतिष्मान्नाम ॥ ४.७.३ ॥

*brahmaṇaḥ somya te pādaṃ bravāṇīti bravītu me bhagavāniti tasmai hovācāgniḥ kalā sūryaḥ kalā candraḥ kalā vidyutkalaiṣa vai somya catuṣkalaḥ pādo brahmaṇo jyotiṣmānnāma || 4.7.3 ||*

The swan said, 'O Somya, I will tell you about one foot of Brahman.' Satyakāma answered, 'Yes, lord, please do.' The swan told him: 'Fire is one part, the sun is a second, the moon is a third, and lightning is a fourth. O Somya, these four parts make up a foot of Brahman. Its name is Jyotiṣmān, the Luminous.'

### 4.7.4

स य एतमेवं विद्वांश्चतुष्कलं पादं ब्रह्मणो ज्योतिष्मानित्युपास्ते ज्योतिष्मानस्मिँल्लोके भवति ज्योतिष्मतो ह लोकाञ्जयति य एतमेवं विद्वांश्चतुष्कलं पादं ब्रह्मणो ज्योतिष्मानित्युपास्ते ॥ ४.७.४ ॥ ॥ इति सप्तमः खण्डः ॥

*sa ya etamevaṃ vidvāṃścatuṣkalaṃ pādaṃ brahmaṇo jyotiṣmānityupāste jyotiṣmānasmim̐lloke bhavati jyotiṣmato ha lokāñjayati ya etamevaṃ vidvāṃścatuṣkalaṃ pādaṃ brahmaṇo jyotiṣmānityupāste || 4.7.4 || || iti saptamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

'Whoever knows this four-part foot of Brahman called "the Luminous" and worships it as that becomes illustrious in this world. Whoever knows this four-part foot of Brahman called "the Luminous" and worships it as that reaches worlds full of light.'

### 4.8.1

मद्गुष्टे पादं वक्तेति स ह श्वोभूते गा अभिप्रस्थापयांचकार ता यत्राभि सायं बभूवुस्तत्राग्निमुपसमाधाय गा उपरुध्य समिधमाधाय पश्चादग्नेः प्राङुपोपविवेश ॥ ४.८.१ ॥

*madguṣṭe pādaṃ vakteti sa ha śvobhūte gā abhiprasthāpayāṃcakāra tā yatrābhi sāyaṃ babhūvustatrāgnimupasamādhāya gā uparudhya samidhamādhāya paścādagneḥ prāṅupopaviveśa || 4.8.1 ||*

The swan then said, 'The madgu will tell you about another foot of Brahman.' The following day Satyakāma gathered the cows and drove them on toward his teacher's house. At dusk they reached a spot where they stopped for the night. He penned the cows, gathered some fuel, lit a fire, and sat down just behind it, facing east.

### 4.8.2

तं मद्गुरुपनिपत्याभ्युवाद सत्यकाम३ इति भगव इति ह प्रतिशुश्राव ॥ ४.८.२ ॥

*taṃ madgurupanipatyābhyuvāda satyakāma3 iti bhagava iti ha pratiśuśrāva || 4.8.2 ||*

A madgu flew down to him and said, 'O Satyakāma.' He answered, 'Yes, lord.'

### 4.8.3

ब्रह्मणः सोम्य ते पादं ब्रवाणीति ब्रवीतु मे भगवानिति तस्मै होवाच प्राणः कला चक्षुः कला श्रोत्रं कला मनः कलैष वै सोम्य चतुष्कलः पादो ब्रह्मण आयतनवान्नाम ॥ ४.८.३ ॥

*brahmaṇaḥ somya te pādaṃ bravāṇīti bravītu me bhagavāniti tasmai hovāca prāṇaḥ kalā cakṣuḥ kalā śrotraṃ kalā manaḥ kalaiṣa vai somya catuṣkalaḥ pādo brahmaṇa āyatanavānnāma || 4.8.3 ||*

The madgu said, 'O Somya, I will tell you about one foot of Brahman.' Satyakāma answered, 'Yes, lord, please do.' The madgu told him: 'Prāṇa is one part, the eyes are a second, the ears are a third, and the mind is a fourth. O Somya, these four parts make up a foot of Brahman. Its name is Āyatanavān, the Support.'

### 4.8.4

स यै एतमेवं विद्वांश्चतुष्कलं पादं ब्रह्मण आयतनवानित्युपास्त आयतनवानस्मिँल्लोके भवत्यायतनवतो ह लोकाञ्जयति य एतमेवं विद्वांश्चतुष्कलं पादं ब्रह्मण आयतनवानित्युपास्ते ॥ ४.८.४ ॥ ॥ इति अष्टमः खण्डः ॥

*sa yai etamevaṃ vidvāṃścatuṣkalaṃ pādaṃ brahmaṇa āyatanavānityupāsta āyatanavānasmim̐lloke bhavatyāyatanavato ha lokāñjayati ya etamevaṃ vidvāṃścatuṣkalaṃ pādaṃ brahmaṇa āyatanavānityupāste || 4.8.4 || || iti aṣṭamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

'Whoever knows this four-part foot of Brahman called "the Support" and worships it as that becomes a support to others in this world. Whoever knows this four-part foot of Brahman called "the Support" and worships it as that reaches spacious worlds.'

### 4.9.1

प्राप हाचर्यकुलं तमाचर्योऽभ्युवाद सत्यकाम३ इति भगव इति ह प्रतिशुश्राव ॥ ४.९.१ ॥

*prāpa hācaryakulaṃ tamācaryo'bhyuvāda satyakāma3 iti bhagava iti ha pratiśuśrāva || 4.9.1 ||*

In time Satyakāma arrived at his teacher's house. The teacher greeted him with 'O Satyakāma.' He answered, 'Yes, lord.'

### 4.9.2

ब्रह्मविदिव वै सोम्य भासि को नु त्वानुशशासेत्यन्ये मनुष्येभ्य इति ह प्रतिजज्ञे भगवांस्त्वेव मे कामे ब्रूयात् ॥ ४.९.२ ॥

*brahmavidiva vai somya bhāsi ko nu tvānuśaśāsetyanye manuṣyebhya iti ha pratijajñe bhagavāṃstveva me kāme brūyāt || 4.9.2 ||*

The teacher said: 'Somya, you glow like someone who has known Brahman. Who taught you?' Satyakāma assured him: 'No human being, certainly. But now, O Lord, will you please teach me about Brahman, the subject dearest to my heart?'

### 4.9.3

श्रुतंह्येव मे भगवद्दृशेभ्य आचार्याद्धैव विद्या विदिता साधिष्ठं प्रापतीति तस्मै हैतदेवोवाचात्र ह न किंचन वीयायेति वीयायेति ॥ ४.९.३ ॥ ॥ इति नवमः खण्डः ॥

*śrutaṃhyeva me bhagavaddṛśebhya ācāryāddhaiva vidyā viditā sādhiṣṭhaṃ prāpatīti tasmai haitadevovācātra ha na kiṃcana vīyāyeti vīyāyeti || 4.9.3 || || iti navamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

Satyakāma said, 'I have heard from revered people like you that one learns best from a competent teacher.' So the teacher taught Satyakāma everything, omitting nothing.

### 4.10.1

उपकोसलो ह वै कामलायनः सत्यकामे जाबाले ब्रह्मचार्यमुवास तस्य ह द्वादश वार्षाण्यग्नीन्परिचचार स ह स्मान्यानन्तेवासिनः समावर्तयंस्तं ह स्मैव न समावर्तयति ॥ ४.१०.१ ॥

*upakosalo ha vai kāmalāyanaḥ satyakāme jābāle brahmacāryamuvāsa tasya ha dvādaśa vārṣāṇyagnīnparicacāra sa ha smānyānantevāsinaḥ samāvartayaṃstaṃ ha smaiva na samāvartayati || 4.10.1 ||*

For twelve years Upakosala Kāmalāyana lived with Satyakāma Jābāla as a celibate disciple, studying the scriptures and tending his teacher's sacrificial fires. When that time was up, the other students were allowed to return home. Upakosala alone was kept back.

### 4.10.2

तं जायोवाच तप्तो ब्रह्मचारी कुशलमग्नीन्परिचचारीन्मा त्वाग्नयः परिप्रवोचन्प्रब्रूह्यस्मा इति तस्मै हाप्रोच्यैव प्रवासांचक्रे ॥ ४.१०.२ ॥

*taṃ jāyovāca tapto brahmacārī kuśalamagnīnparicacārīnmā tvāgnayaḥ paripravocanprabrūhyasmā iti tasmai hāprocyaiva pravāsāṃcakre || 4.10.2 ||*

Satyakāma's wife said to him: 'This brahmacārin has grown very weak from austerities and has tended the fires with great care. Teach him, or the fires may blame you.' But instead of teaching him, Satyakāma went away on a journey.

### 4.10.3

स ह व्याधिनानशितुं दध्रे तमाचार्यजायोवाच ब्रह्मचारिन्नशान किं नु नाश्नासीति स होवाच बहव इमेऽस्मिन्पुरुषे कामा नानात्यया व्याधीभिः प्रतिपूर्णोऽस्मि नाशिष्यामीति ॥ ४.१०.३ ॥

*sa ha vyādhinānaśituṃ dadhre tamācāryajāyovāca brahmacārinnaśāna kiṃ nu nāśnāsīti sa hovāca bahava ime'sminpuruṣe kāmā nānātyayā vyādhībhiḥ pratipūrṇo'smi nāśiṣyāmīti || 4.10.3 ||*

Upakosala was distressed and began to fast. His teacher's wife said to him: 'Brahmacārin, have something to eat. Why are you not eating?' Upakosala said: 'I have too many desires in me, dragging me in different directions. I am like someone suffering from many illnesses. I do not want food.'

### 4.10.4

अथ हाग्नयः समूदिरे तप्तो ब्रह्मचारी कुशलं नः पर्यचारीद्धन्तास्मै प्रब्रवामेति तस्मै होचुः प्राणो ब्रह्म कं ब्रह्म खं ब्रह्मेति ॥ ४.१०.४ ॥

*atha hāgnayaḥ samūdire tapto brahmacārī kuśalaṃ naḥ paryacārīddhantāsmai prabravāmeti tasmai hocuḥ prāṇo brahma kaṃ brahma khaṃ brahmeti || 4.10.4 ||*

The fires, the Dakṣiṇāgni, the Gārhapatya, and the Āhavanīya, then began saying to one another: 'This brahmacārin has grown thin from his austerities, and for a long time he has tended us with great care. Let us teach him.' To Upakosala they said, 'Prāṇa is Brahman, ka (happiness) is Brahman, and kha (space) is Brahman.'

### 4.10.5

स होवाच विजानाम्यहं यत्प्राणो ब्रह्म कं च तु खं च न विजानामीति ते होचुर्यद्वाव कं तदेव खं यदेव खं तदेव कमिति प्राणं च हास्मै तदाकाशं चोचुः ॥ ४.१०.५ ॥ ॥ इति दशमः खण्डः ॥

*sa hovāca vijānāmyahaṃ yatprāṇo brahma kaṃ ca tu khaṃ ca na vijānāmīti te hocuryadvāva kaṃ tadeva khaṃ yadeva khaṃ tadeva kamiti prāṇaṃ ca hāsmai tadākāśaṃ cocuḥ || 4.10.5 || || iti daśamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

Upakosala said: 'I understand that prāṇa is Brahman. But I do not understand that ka and kha are Brahman.' The fires answered, 'What is ka is also kha, and what is kha is also ka.' Then the fires taught him that Brahman was both prāṇa and ākāśa (space).

### 4.11.1

अथ हैनं गार्हपत्योऽनुशशास पृथिव्यग्निरन्नमादित्य इति य एष आदित्ये पुरुषो दृश्यते सोऽहमस्मि स एवाहमस्मीति ॥ ४.११.१ ॥

*atha hainaṃ gārhapatyo'nuśaśāsa pṛthivyagnirannamāditya iti ya eṣa āditye puruṣo dṛśyate so'hamasmi sa evāhamasmīti || 4.11.1 ||*

After this the Gārhapatya fire said to Upakosala: 'Earth, fire, food, and the sun all belong to my body, that is, to Brahman's body. The person seen in the disc of the sun is me. I am that.'

### 4.11.2

स य एतमेवं विद्वानुपास्तेऽपहते पापकृत्यां लोकी भवति सर्वमायुरेति ज्योग्जीवति नास्यावरपुरुषाः क्षीयन्त उप वयं तं भुञ्जामोऽस्मिंश्च लोकेऽमुष्मिंश्च य एतमेवं विद्वानुपास्ते ॥ ४.११.२ ॥ ॥ इति एकादशः खण्डः ॥

*sa ya etamevaṃ vidvānupāste'pahate pāpakṛtyāṃ lokī bhavati sarvamāyureti jyogjīvati nāsyāvarapuruṣāḥ kṣīyanta upa vayaṃ taṃ bhuñjāmo'smiṃśca loke'muṣmiṃśca ya etamevaṃ vidvānupāste || 4.11.2 || || iti ekādaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

'Whoever knows this Gārhapatya fire and worships it in this way is rid of all his sins and reaches the world of the Gārhapatya fire. His life is long and bright, and his line does not die out. In this world and the next, we watch over the one who knows this fire and worships it in this way.'

### 4.12.1

अथ हैनमन्वाहार्यपचनोऽनुशशासापो दिशो नक्षत्राणि चन्द्रमा इति य एष चन्द्रमसि पुरुषो दृश्यते सोऽहमस्मि स एवाहमस्मीति ॥ ४.१२.१ ॥

*atha hainamanvāhāryapacano'nuśaśāsāpo diśo nakṣatrāṇi candramā iti ya eṣa candramasi puruṣo dṛśyate so'hamasmi sa evāhamasmīti || 4.12.1 ||*

Next the Dakṣiṇāgni (Southern) fire said to Upakosala: 'Water, the quarters, the stars, and the moon all belong to my body, that is, to Brahman's body. The person seen in the moon is me. I am that.'

### 4.12.2

स य एतमेवं विद्वानुपास्तेऽपहते पापकृत्यां लोकी भवति सर्वमायुरेति ज्योग्जीवति नास्यावरपुरुषाः क्षीयन्त उप वयं तं भुञ्जामोऽस्मिंश्च लोकेऽमुष्मिंश्च य एतमेवं विद्वानुपास्ते ॥ ४.१२.२ ॥ ॥ इति द्वादशः खण्डः ॥

*sa ya etamevaṃ vidvānupāste'pahate pāpakṛtyāṃ lokī bhavati sarvamāyureti jyogjīvati nāsyāvarapuruṣāḥ kṣīyanta upa vayaṃ taṃ bhuñjāmo'smiṃśca loke'muṣmiṃśca ya etamevaṃ vidvānupāste || 4.12.2 || || iti dvādaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

'Whoever knows this Dakṣiṇāgni fire and worships it in this way is rid of all his sins and reaches the world of the Dakṣiṇāgni fire. His life is long and bright, and his line does not die out. In this world and the next, we watch over the one who knows this fire and worships it in this way.'

### 4.13.1

अथ हैनमाहवनीयोऽनुशशास प्राण आकाशो द्यौर्विद्युदिति य एष विद्युति पुरुषो दृश्यते सोऽहमस्मि स एवाहमस्मीति ॥ ४.१३.१ ॥

*atha hainamāhavanīyo'nuśaśāsa prāṇa ākāśo dyaurvidyuditi ya eṣa vidyuti puruṣo dṛśyate so'hamasmi sa evāhamasmīti || 4.13.1 ||*

Next the Āhavanīya fire said to Upakosala: 'Prāṇa, space, heaven, and lightning all belong to my body, that is, to Brahman's body. The person seen in lightning is me. I am that.'

### 4.13.2

स य एतमेवं विद्वानुपास्तेऽपहते पापकृत्यां लोकी भवति सर्वमयुरेति ज्योग्जीवति नास्यावरपुरुषाः क्षीयन्त उप वयं तं भुञ्जामोऽस्मिंश्च लोकेऽमुष्मिंश्च य एतमेवं विद्वानुपास्ते ॥ ४.१३.२ ॥ ॥ इति त्रयोदशः खण्डः ॥

*sa ya etamevaṃ vidvānupāste'pahate pāpakṛtyāṃ lokī bhavati sarvamayureti jyogjīvati nāsyāvarapuruṣāḥ kṣīyanta upa vayaṃ taṃ bhuñjāmo'smiṃśca loke'muṣmiṃśca ya etamevaṃ vidvānupāste || 4.13.2 || || iti trayodaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

'Whoever knows this Āhavanīya fire and worships it in this way is rid of all his sins and reaches the world of the Āhavanīya fire. His life is long and bright, and his line does not die out. In this world and the next, we watch over the one who knows this fire and worships it in this way.'

### 4.14.1

ते होचुरुपकोसलैषा सोम्य तेऽस्मद्विद्यात्मविद्या चाचार्यस्तु ते गतिं वक्तेत्याजगाम हास्याचार्यस्तमाचार्योऽभ्युवादोपकोसल३ इति ॥ ४.१४.१ ॥

*te hocurupakosalaiṣā somya te'smadvidyātmavidyā cācāryastu te gatiṃ vaktetyājagāma hāsyācāryastamācāryo'bhyuvādopakosala3 iti || 4.14.1 ||*

The fires said: 'O Somya Upakosala, we have now given you the knowledge of fire, which is also Self-knowledge. Your teacher will explain the way to the next world.' In time his teacher came back and called to him, 'Upakosala.'

### 4.14.2

भगव इति ह प्रतिशुश्राव ब्रह्मविद इव सोम्य ते मुखं भाति को नु त्वानुशशासेति को नु मानुशिष्याद्भो इतीहापेव निह्नुत इमे नूनमीदृशा अन्यादृशा इतीहाग्नीनभ्यूदे किं नु सोम्य किल तेऽवोचन्निति ॥ ४.१४.२ ॥

*bhagava iti ha pratiśuśrāva brahmavida iva somya te mukhaṃ bhāti ko nu tvānuśaśāseti ko nu mānuśiṣyādbho itīhāpeva nihnuta ime nūnamīdṛśā anyādṛśā itīhāgnīnabhyūde kiṃ nu somya kila te'vocanniti || 4.14.2 ||*

Upakosala answered, 'Yes, lord.' His teacher said: 'O Somya, your face glows like that of a knower of Brahman. Who has taught you?' 'Sir, who would teach me?' he said, as if trying to conceal the truth. Then he pointed to the fires and said: 'They looked different before. Now they look like this.' In this way he indicated the fires. The teacher asked, 'Somya, what did the fires teach you?'

### 4.14.3

इदमिति ह प्रतिजज्ञे लोकान्वाव किल सोम्य तेऽवोचन्नहं तु ते तद्वक्ष्यामि यथा पुष्करपलाश आपो न श्लिष्यन्त एवमेवंविदि पापं कर्म न श्लिष्यत इति ब्रवीतु मे भगवानिति तस्मै होवाच ॥ ४.१४.३ ॥ ॥ इति चतुर्दशः खण्डः ॥

*idamiti ha pratijajñe lokānvāva kila somya te'vocannahaṃ tu te tadvakṣyāmi yathā puṣkarapalāśa āpo na śliṣyanta evamevaṃvidi pāpaṃ karma na śliṣyata iti bravītu me bhagavāniti tasmai hovāca || 4.14.3 || || iti caturdaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

Upakosala said, 'This is what they told me,' and he reported to his teacher all that the fires had taught him. The teacher said: 'O Somya, they taught you only about the worlds, but I will teach you about Brahman.'

### 4.15.1

य एषोऽक्षिणि पुरुषो दृश्यत एष आत्मेति होवाचैतदमृतमभयमेतद्ब्रह्मेति तद्यद्यप्यस्मिन्सर्पिर्वोदकं वा सिञ्चति वर्त्मनी एव गच्छति ॥ ४.१५.१ ॥

*ya eṣo'kṣiṇi puruṣo dṛśyata eṣa ātmeti hovācaitadamṛtamabhayametadbrahmeti tadyadyapyasminsarpirvodakaṃ vā siñcati vartmanī eva gacchati || 4.15.1 ||*

The teacher said: 'The person seen in the eyes is the Self. It is immortal and fearless. It is Brahman. That is why clarified butter or water, if put in the eyes, runs off to their corners.'

### 4.15.2

एतं संयद्वाम इत्याचक्षत एतं हि सर्वाणि वामान्यभिसंयन्ति सर्वाण्येनं वामान्यभिसंयन्ति य एवं वेद ॥ ४.१५.२ ॥

*etaṃ saṃyadvāma ityācakṣata etaṃ hi sarvāṇi vāmānyabhisaṃyanti sarvāṇyenaṃ vāmānyabhisaṃyanti ya evaṃ veda || 4.15.2 ||*

He is called Saṃyadvāma, because everything good and beautiful comes to him. Everything good and beautiful comes also to the one who knows this.

### 4.15.3

एष उ एव वामनीरेष हि सर्वाणि वामानि नयति सर्वाणि वामानि नयति य एवं वेद ॥ ४.१५.३ ॥

*eṣa u eva vāmanīreṣa hi sarvāṇi vāmāni nayati sarvāṇi vāmāni nayati ya evaṃ veda || 4.15.3 ||*

This person in the eyes is Vāmanī, the source of all that is good and pure, because he inspires everything good and pure in people. The one who knows this bestows all that is good and pure on others.

### 4.15.4

एष उ एव भामनीरेष हि सर्वेषु लोकेषु भाति सर्वेषु लोकेषु भाति य एवं वेद ॥ ४.१५.४ ॥

*eṣa u eva bhāmanīreṣa hi sarveṣu lokeṣu bhāti sarveṣu lokeṣu bhāti ya evaṃ veda || 4.15.4 ||*

The person in the eyes is Bhāmanī, the Shining, because he shines in all the worlds, the sun included. The one who knows this shines in all the worlds.

### 4.15.5

अथ यदु चैवास्मिञ्छव्यं कुर्वन्ति यदि च नार्चिषमेवाभिसंभवन्त्यर्चिषोऽहरह्न आपूर्यमाणपक्षमापूर्यमाणपक्षाद्यान्षडुदङ्ङेति मासांस्तान्मासेभ्यः संवत्सरं संवत्सरादादित्यमादित्याच्चन्द्रमसं चन्द्रमसो विद्युतं तत् पुरुषोऽमानवः स एनान्ब्रह्म गमयत्येष देवपथो ब्रह्मपथ एतेन प्रतिपद्यमाना इमं मानवमावर्तं नावर्तन्ते नावर्तन्ते ॥ ४.१५.५ ॥ ॥ इति पञ्चदशः खण्डः ॥

*atha yadu caivāsmiñchavyaṃ kurvanti yadi ca nārciṣamevābhisaṃbhavantyarciṣo'harahna āpūryamāṇapakṣamāpūryamāṇapakṣādyānṣaḍudaṅṅeti māsāṃstānmāsebhyaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ saṃvatsarādādityamādityāccandramasaṃ candramaso vidyutaṃ tat puruṣo'mānavaḥ sa enānbrahma gamayatyeṣa devapatho brahmapatha etena pratipadyamānā imaṃ mānavamāvartaṃ nāvartante nāvartante || 4.15.5 || || iti pañcadaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

Then those who know this, whether or not proper funeral rites are done for them, go after death to the world of light. From the world of light they pass to the world of day, from there to the world of the bright fortnight, from there to the six months of the sun's northward course, from there to the year, from the year to the sun, from the sun to the moon, and from the moon to lightning. There a being who is not human meets them and leads them to brahmaloka. This is the way of the gods, and it is also the way to Brahman. Those who travel this path never come back to this mortal world. They never come back.

### 4.16.1

एष ह वै यज्ञो योऽयं पवते एष ह यन्निदं सर्वं पुनाति यदेष यन्निदं सर्वं पुनाति तस्मादेष एव यज्ञस्तस्य मनश्च वाक्च वर्तनी ॥ ४.१६.१ ॥

*eṣa ha vai yajño yo'yaṃ pavate eṣa ha yannidaṃ sarvaṃ punāti yadeṣa yannidaṃ sarvaṃ punāti tasmādeṣa eva yajñastasya manaśca vākca vartanī || 4.16.1 ||*

The one who blows, that is, the air, is the sacrifice. As he moves he purifies all this, and because he purifies all this as he moves, he is the sacrifice. Mind and speech are his two paths.

### 4.16.2

तयोरन्यतरां मनसा संस्करोति ब्रह्मा वाचा होताध्वर्युरुद्गातान्यतरांस यत्रौपाकृते प्रातरनुवाके पुरा परिधानीयाया ब्रह्मा व्यवदति ॥ ४.१६.२ ॥ अन्यतरामेव वर्तनीं संस्करोति हीयतेऽन्यतरा स यथैकपाद्व्रजन्रथो वैकेन चक्रेण वर्तमानो रिष्यत्येवमस्य यज्ञोरिष्यति यज्ञं रिष्यन्तं यजमानोऽनुरिष्यति स इष्ट्वा पापीयान्भवति ॥ ४.१६.३ ॥

*tayoranyatarāṃ manasā saṃskaroti brahmā vācā hotādhvaryurudgātānyatarāṃsa yatraupākṛte prātaranuvāke purā paridhānīyāyā brahmā vyavadati || 4.16.2 || anyatarāmeva vartanīṃ saṃskaroti hīyate'nyatarā sa yathaikapādvrajanratho vaikena cakreṇa vartamāno riṣyatyevamasya yajñoriṣyati yajñaṃ riṣyantaṃ yajamāno'nuriṣyati sa iṣṭvā pāpīyānbhavati || 4.16.3 ||*

The priest of a sacrifice called the brahmā purifies one of these two paths, the path of the mind, with his discriminating mind. The hotā, adhvaryu, and udgātā priests purify the other, the path of speech, with chaste and elegant speech. But if the brahmā priest breaks his silence after the morning anuvāka has begun and before the paridhānīya Ṛk hymn has been read, only one path, the path of speech, has been purified, and the other is ruined. Just as a person with one leg trying to walk or a chariot with one wheel trying to move comes to grief, so the sacrifice is ruined. When the sacrifice is ruined, the sacrificer is ruined with it. Indeed, he even incurs sin for having performed the sacrifice in that way.

### 4.16.4

अथ यत्रोपाकृते प्रातरनुवाके न पुरा परिधानीयाया ब्रह्मा व्यवदत्युभे एव वर्तनी संस्कुर्वन्ति न हीयतेऽन्यतरा ॥ ४.१६.४ ॥

*atha yatropākṛte prātaranuvāke na purā paridhānīyāyā brahmā vyavadatyubhe eva vartanī saṃskurvanti na hīyate'nyatarā || 4.16.4 ||*

But when the morning anuvāka has begun in a sacrifice and the brahmā priest keeps his silence until the paridhānīya has started, both paths are purified, and neither is destroyed.

### 4.16.5

स यथोभयपाद्व्रजन्रथो वोभाभ्यां चक्राभ्यां वर्तमानः प्रतितिष्ठत्येवमस्य यज्ञः प्रतितिष्ठति यज्ञं प्रतितिष्ठन्तं यजमानोऽनुप्रतितिष्ठति स इष्ट्वा श्रेयान्भवति ॥ ४.१६.५ ॥ ॥ इति षोडशः खण्डः ॥

*sa yathobhayapādvrajanratho vobhābhyāṃ cakrābhyāṃ vartamānaḥ pratitiṣṭhatyevamasya yajñaḥ pratitiṣṭhati yajñaṃ pratitiṣṭhantaṃ yajamāno'nupratitiṣṭhati sa iṣṭvā śreyānbhavati || 4.16.5 || || iti ṣoḍaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

Just as someone with two legs can walk, or a chariot with two wheels can move, and reach the goal, so his sacrifice succeeds. When the sacrifice succeeds, the sacrificer succeeds too, and through his sacrifice he gains great stature.

### 4.17.1

प्रजापतिर्लोकानभ्यतपत्तेषां तप्यमानानां रसान्प्रावृहदग्निं पृथिव्या वायुमन्तरिक्षातादित्यं दिवः ॥ ४.१७.१ ॥

*prajāpatirlokānabhyatapatteṣāṃ tapyamānānāṃ rasānprāvṛhadagniṃ pṛthivyā vāyumantarikṣātādityaṃ divaḥ || 4.17.1 ||*

Prajāpati worshipped the worlds and managed to draw the essence out of those he worshipped. From earth he drew fire, from the interspace air, and from heaven the sun.

### 4.17.2

स एतास्तिस्रो देवता अभ्यतपत्तासां तप्यमानानां रसान्प्रावृहदग्नेरृचो वायोर्यजूंषि सामान्यादित्यात् ॥ ४.१७.२ ॥

*sa etāstisro devatā abhyatapattāsāṃ tapyamānānāṃ rasānprāvṛhadagnerṛco vāyoryajūṃṣi sāmānyādityāt || 4.17.2 ||*

Next he worshipped these three deities and drew the essence out of them. From fire he obtained the Ṛk mantras, from air the Yajuḥ mantras, and from the sun the Sāma mantras.

### 4.17.3

स एतां त्रयीं विद्यामभ्यतपत्तस्यास्तप्यमानाया रसान्प्रावृहद्भूरित्यृग्भ्यो भुवरिति यजुर्भ्यः स्वरिति सामभ्यः ॥ ४.१७.३ ॥

*sa etāṃ trayīṃ vidyāmabhyatapattasyāstapyamānāyā rasānprāvṛhadbhūrityṛgbhyo bhuvariti yajurbhyaḥ svariti sāmabhyaḥ || 4.17.3 ||*

Next Prajāpati worshipped these three Vedas and drew the essence out of them. From the Ṛg Veda he obtained 'bhūḥ,' from the Yajur Veda 'bhuvaḥ,' and from the Sāma Veda 'svaḥ.'

### 4.17.4

तद्यदृक्तो रिष्येद्भूः स्वाहेति गार्हपत्ये जुहुयादृचामेव तद्रसेनर्चां वीर्येणर्चां यज्ञस्य विरिष्टं संदधाति ॥ ४.१७.४ ॥

*tadyadṛkto riṣyedbhūḥ svāheti gārhapatye juhuyādṛcāmeva tadrasenarcāṃ vīryeṇarcāṃ yajñasya viriṣṭaṃ saṃdadhāti || 4.17.4 ||*

For this reason, if a mistake in the Ṛk seems likely to harm the sacrifice, the priest should make an offering in the Gārhapatya fire, saying, 'Bhūḥ svāhā.' Then the essence and strength of the Ṛk will avert any harm that a mistake in the Ṛk was likely to do to the sacrifice.

### 4.17.5

स यदि यजुष्टो रिष्येद्भुवः स्वाहेति दक्षिणाग्नौ जुहुयाद्यजुषामेव तद्रसेन यजुषां वीर्येण यजुषां यज्ञस्य विरिष्टं संदधाति ॥ ४.१७.५ ॥

*sa yadi yajuṣṭo riṣyedbhuvaḥ svāheti dakṣiṇāgnau juhuyādyajuṣāmeva tadrasena yajuṣāṃ vīryeṇa yajuṣāṃ yajñasya viriṣṭaṃ saṃdadhāti || 4.17.5 ||*

Then, if a mistake in the Yajuḥ seems likely to harm the sacrifice, the priest should make an offering in the Dakṣiṇāgni fire, saying, 'Bhuvaḥ svāhā.' Then the essence and strength of the Yajuḥ will avert any harm that a mistake in the Yajuḥ was likely to do to the sacrifice.

### 4.17.6

अथ यदि सामतो रिष्येत्स्वः स्वाहेत्याहवनीये जुहुयात्साम्नामेव तद्रसेन साम्नां वीर्येण साम्नां यज्ञस्य विरिष्टं संदधाति ॥ ४.१७.६ ॥

*atha yadi sāmato riṣyetsvaḥ svāhetyāhavanīye juhuyātsāmnāmeva tadrasena sāmnāṃ vīryeṇa sāmnāṃ yajñasya viriṣṭaṃ saṃdadhāti || 4.17.6 ||*

Then, if a mistake in the Sāma seems likely to harm the sacrifice, the priest should make an offering in the Āhavanīya fire, saying, 'Svaḥ svāhā.' Then the essence and strength of the Sāma will avert any harm that a mistake in the Sāma was likely to do to the sacrifice.

### 4.17.7

तद्यथा लवणेन सुवर्णं संदध्यात्सुवर्णेन रजतं रजतेन त्रपु त्रपुणा सीसं सीसेन लोहं लोहेन दारु दारु चर्मणा ॥ ४.१७.७ ॥

*tadyathā lavaṇena suvarṇaṃ saṃdadhyātsuvarṇena rajataṃ rajatena trapu trapuṇā sīsaṃ sīsena lohaṃ lohena dāru dāru carmaṇā || 4.17.7 ||*

It is like using borax to join gold, gold to join silver, silver to join tin, tin to join lead, lead to join iron, iron to join wood, and leather to join wood.

### 4.17.8

एवमेषां लोकानामासां देवतानामस्यास्त्रय्या विद्याया वीर्येण यज्ञस्य विरिष्टं संदधाति भेषजकृतो ह वा एष यज्ञो यत्रैवंविद्ब्रह्मा भवति ॥ ४.१७.८ ॥

*evameṣāṃ lokānāmāsāṃ devatānāmasyāstrayyā vidyāyā vīryeṇa yajñasya viriṣṭaṃ saṃdadhāti bheṣajakṛto ha vā eṣa yajño yatraivaṃvidbrahmā bhavati || 4.17.8 ||*

In the same way, the power of these worlds, these deities, and these three holy scriptures makes good any flaws in the sacrifice. A sacrifice with a knowledgeable brahmā priest receives the right medicine.

### 4.17.9

एष ह वा उदक्प्रवणो यज्ञो यत्रैवंविद्ब्रह्मा भवत्येवंविदं ह वा एषा ब्रह्माणमनुगाथा यतो यत आवर्तते तत्तद्गच्छति ॥ ४.१७.९ ॥

*eṣa ha vā udakpravaṇo yajño yatraivaṃvidbrahmā bhavatyevaṃvidaṃ ha vā eṣā brahmāṇamanugāthā yato yata āvartate tattadgacchati || 4.17.9 ||*

A sacrifice guided by a capable brahmā priest leads to the uttarāyaṇa (the path of the gods). A verse praises such a learned priest: 'Wherever the sacrifice goes astray, this priest goes there to put things right.'

### 4.17.10

मानवो ब्रह्मैवैक ऋत्विक्कुरूनश्वाभिरक्षत्येवंविद्ध वै ब्रह्मा यज्ञं यजमानं सर्वांश्चर्त्विजोऽभिरक्षति तस्मादेवंविदमेव ब्रह्माणं कुर्वीत नानेवंविदं नानेवंविदम् ॥ ४.१७.१० ॥ ॥ इति चतुर्थोऽध्यायः ॥

*mānavo brahmaivaika ṛtvikkurūnaśvābhirakṣatyevaṃviddha vai brahmā yajñaṃ yajamānaṃ sarvāṃścartvijo'bhirakṣati tasmādevaṃvidameva brahmāṇaṃ kurvīta nānevaṃvidaṃ nānevaṃvidam || 4.17.10 || || iti caturtho'dhyāyaḥ ||*

A good brahmā priest is one who can keep silent, or one who is thoughtful. As a horse protects the soldiers, a learned brahmā priest protects the sacrifice, the sacrifices, and all the other priests. So appoint only a learned brahmā like this for your sacrifice, and no one else.

### 5.1.1

॥ पञ्चमोऽध्यायः ॥ यो ह वै ज्येष्ठं च श्रेष्ठं च वेद ज्येष्ठश्च ह वै श्रेष्ठश्च भवति प्राणो वाव ज्येष्ठश्च श्रेष्ठश्च ॥ ५.१.१ ॥

*|| pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ || yo ha vai jyeṣṭhaṃ ca śreṣṭhaṃ ca veda jyeṣṭhaśca ha vai śreṣṭhaśca bhavati prāṇo vāva jyeṣṭhaśca śreṣṭhaśca || 5.1.1 ||*

Om. Whoever knows the oldest and the best becomes the oldest and the best himself. Prāṇa is what is oldest and best.

### 5.1.2

यो ह वै वसिष्ठं वेद वसिष्ठो ह स्वानां भवति वाग्वाव वसिष्ठः ॥ ५.१.२ ॥

*yo ha vai vasiṣṭhaṃ veda vasiṣṭho ha svānāṃ bhavati vāgvāva vasiṣṭhaḥ || 5.1.2 ||*

Whoever knows what is of high standing rises to high standing himself among his own relatives. Eloquence brings one this high standing in society.

### 5.1.3

यो ह वै प्रतिष्ठां वेद प्रति ह तिष्ठत्यस्मिंश्च लोकेऽमुष्मिंश्च चक्षुर्वाव प्रतिष्ठा ॥ ५.१.३ ॥

*yo ha vai pratiṣṭhāṃ veda prati ha tiṣṭhatyasmiṃśca loke'muṣmiṃśca cakṣurvāva pratiṣṭhā || 5.1.3 ||*

Whoever knows the support gains a support in this world and in the other world, heaven. The eye is indeed the support.

### 5.1.4

यो ह वै सम्पदं वेद संहास्मै कामाः पद्यन्ते दैवाश्च मानुषाश्च श्रोत्रं वाव सम्पत् ॥ ५.१.४ ॥

*yo ha vai sampadaṃ veda saṃhāsmai kāmāḥ padyante daivāśca mānuṣāśca śrotraṃ vāva sampat || 5.1.4 ||*

Whoever knows affluence has everything that humans and gods desire come to him. The ears stand for affluence.

### 5.1.5

यो ह वा आयतनं वेदायतनं ह स्वानां भवति मनो ह वा आयतनम् ॥ ५.१.५ ॥

*yo ha vā āyatanaṃ vedāyatanaṃ ha svānāṃ bhavati mano ha vā āyatanam || 5.1.5 ||*

The mind is the abode. A person who knows the abode becomes a shelter for his family.

### 5.1.6

अथ ह प्राणा अहंश्रेयसि व्यूदिरेऽहंश्रेयानस्म्यहं श्रेयानस्मीति ॥ ५.१.६ ॥

*atha ha prāṇā ahaṃśreyasi vyūdire'haṃśreyānasmyahaṃ śreyānasmīti || 5.1.6 ||*

One day the sense organs started arguing with one another, each insisting it was supreme. Every one of them kept saying, "I am the best. I am the best."

### 5.1.7

ते ह प्राणाः प्रजापतिं पितरमेत्योचुर्भगवन्को नः श्रेष्ठ इति तान्होवाच यस्मिन्व उत्क्रान्ते शरीरं पापिष्ठतरमिव दृश्येत स वः श्रेष्ठ इति ॥ ५.१.७ ॥

*te ha prāṇāḥ prajāpatiṃ pitarametyocurbhagavanko naḥ śreṣṭha iti tānhovāca yasminva utkrānte śarīraṃ pāpiṣṭhataramiva dṛśyeta sa vaḥ śreṣṭha iti || 5.1.7 ||*

So the organs went to their father Prajāpati and asked, "Revered sir, which of us is the best?" He answered, "The best among you is the one whose departure leaves the body completely untouchable."

### 5.1.8

सा ह वागुच्चक्राम सा संवत्सरं प्रोष्य पर्येत्योवाच कथमशकतर्ते मज्जीवितुमिति यथा कला अवदन्तः प्राणन्तः प्राणेन पश्यन्तश्चक्षुषा शृण्वन्तः श्रोत्रेण ध्यायन्तो मनसैवमिति प्रविवेश ह वाक् ॥ ५.१.८ ॥

*sā ha vāguccakrāma sā saṃvatsaraṃ proṣya paryetyovāca kathamaśakatarte majjīvitumiti yathā kalā avadantaḥ prāṇantaḥ prāṇena paśyantaścakṣuṣā śṛṇvantaḥ śrotreṇa dhyāyanto manasaivamiti praviveśa ha vāk || 5.1.8 ||*

Speech was the first to leave the body. A full year later he returned and asked the other organs, "How did you keep yourselves going without me?" They answered, "We lived as mute people do. They do not speak, yet they survive by breathing, see with their eyes, hear with their ears and think with their minds. We did likewise." On hearing this, speech entered the body again.

### 5.1.9

चक्षुर्होच्चक्राम तत्संवत्सरं प्रोष्य पर्येत्योवाच कथमशकतर्ते मज्जीवितुमिति यथान्धा अपश्यन्तः प्राणन्तः प्राणेन वदन्तो वाचा शृण्वन्तः श्रोत्रेण ध्यायन्तो मनसैवमिति प्रविवेश ह चक्षुः ॥ ५.१.९ ॥

*cakṣurhoccakrāma tatsaṃvatsaraṃ proṣya paryetyovāca kathamaśakatarte majjīvitumiti yathāndhā apaśyantaḥ prāṇantaḥ prāṇena vadanto vācā śṛṇvantaḥ śrotreṇa dhyāyanto manasaivamiti praviveśa ha cakṣuḥ || 5.1.9 ||*

The organ of vision left next. A full year later it returned and asked the other organs, "How did you keep yourselves going without me?" They answered, "We lived as blind people do. They do not see, yet they survive by breathing, speak with the organ of speech, hear with their ears and think with their minds. We did likewise." On hearing this, the organ of vision entered the body again.

### 5.1.10

श्रोत्रं होच्चक्राम तत्संवत्सरं प्रोष्य पर्येत्योवाच कथमशकतर्ते मज्जीवितुमिति यथा बधिरा अशृण्वन्तः प्राणन्तः प्राणेन वदन्तो वाचा पश्यन्तश्चक्षुषा ध्यायन्तो मनसैवमिति प्रविवेश ह श्रोत्रम् ॥ ५.१.१० ॥

*śrotraṃ hoccakrāma tatsaṃvatsaraṃ proṣya paryetyovāca kathamaśakatarte majjīvitumiti yathā badhirā aśṛṇvantaḥ prāṇantaḥ prāṇena vadanto vācā paśyantaścakṣuṣā dhyāyanto manasaivamiti praviveśa ha śrotram || 5.1.10 ||*

The organ of hearing left next. A full year later it returned and asked the other organs, "How did you keep yourselves going without me?" They answered, "We lived as deaf people do. They do not hear, yet they survive by breathing, speak with the organ of speech, see with their eyes and think with their minds. We did likewise." On hearing this, the organ of hearing entered the body again.

### 5.1.11

मनो होच्चक्राम तत्संवत्सरं प्रोष्य पर्येत्योवाच कथमशकतर्ते मज्जीवितुमिति यथा बाला अमनसः प्राणन्तः प्राणेन वदन्तो वाचा पश्यन्तश्चक्षुषा शृण्वन्तः श्रोत्रेणैवमिति प्रविवेश ह मनः ॥ ५.१.११ ॥

*mano hoccakrāma tatsaṃvatsaraṃ proṣya paryetyovāca kathamaśakatarte majjīvitumiti yathā bālā amanasaḥ prāṇantaḥ prāṇena vadanto vācā paśyantaścakṣuṣā śṛṇvantaḥ śrotreṇaivamiti praviveśa ha manaḥ || 5.1.11 ||*

The mind left next. A full year later he returned and asked the other organs, "How did you keep yourselves going without me?" They answered, "We lived as children do. They do not think for themselves, yet they survive by breathing, speak with the organ of speech, see with their eyes and hear with their ears. We did likewise." On hearing this, the mind entered the body again.

### 5.1.12

अथ ह प्राण उच्चिक्रमिषन्स यथा सुहयः पड्वीशशङ्कून्संखिदेदेवमितरान्प्राणान्समखिदत्तं हाभिसमेत्योचुर्भगवन्नेधि त्वं नः श्रेष्ठोऽसि मोत्क्रमीरिति ॥ ५.१.१२ ॥

*atha ha prāṇa uccikramiṣansa yathā suhayaḥ paḍvīśaśaṅkūnsaṃkhidedevamitarānprāṇānsamakhidattaṃ hābhisametyocurbhagavannedhi tvaṃ naḥ śreṣṭho'si motkramīriti || 5.1.12 ||*

Then prāṇa, the vital force, made up its mind to go. Just as a fine horse can pull up the pegs its feet are tethered to, the chief prāṇa was on the point of dragging the other organs off with it. The other organs came to it in great humility and said, "Lord, be our leader. You are the greatest of us. Please do not leave us."

### 5.1.13

अथ हैनं वागुवाच यदहं वसिष्ठोऽस्मि त्वं तद्वसिष्ठोऽसीत्यथ हैनं चक्षुरुवाच यदहं प्रतिष्ठास्मि त्वं तत्प्रतिष्ठासीति ॥ ५.१.१३ ॥

*atha hainaṃ vāguvāca yadahaṃ vasiṣṭho'smi tvaṃ tadvasiṣṭho'sītyatha hainaṃ cakṣuruvāca yadahaṃ pratiṣṭhāsmi tvaṃ tatpratiṣṭhāsīti || 5.1.13 ||*

Then the organ of speech told the chief prāṇa, "If I possess the quality of high standing, it is because you possess it." The organ of vision next told it, "Yes, I have the quality of supporting others, but I owe that to you."

### 5.1.14

अथ हैनंश्रोत्रमुवाच यदहं सम्पदस्मि त्वं तत्सम्पदसीत्यथ हैनं मन उवाच यदहमायतनमस्मि त्वं तदायतनमसीति ॥ ५.१.१४ ॥

*atha hainaṃśrotramuvāca yadahaṃ sampadasmi tvaṃ tatsampadasītyatha hainaṃ mana uvāca yadahamāyatanamasmi tvaṃ tadāyatanamasīti || 5.1.14 ||*

Then the organ of hearing told the chief prāṇa, "If I possess the quality of affluence, it is because you possess it." The mind next told it, "Yes, I have the quality of sheltering many, but in truth that quality is yours."

### 5.1.15

न वै वाचो न चक्षूंषि न श्रोत्राणि न मनांसीत्याचक्षते प्राणा इत्येवाचक्षते प्राणो ह्येवैतानि सर्वाणि भवति ॥ ५.१.१५ ॥ ॥ इति प्रथमः खण्डः ॥

*na vai vāco na cakṣūṃṣi na śrotrāṇi na manāṃsītyācakṣate prāṇā ityevācakṣate prāṇo hyevaitāni sarvāṇi bhavati || 5.1.15 || || iti prathamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

Scholars do not call these organs speech, eyes, ears or minds. They call them "prāṇas," because prāṇa has become all of these organs.

### 5.2.1

स होवाच किं मेऽन्नं भविष्यतीति यत्किंचिदिदमा श्वभ्य आ शकुनिभ्य इति होचुस्तद्वा एतदनस्यान्नमनो ह वै नाम प्रत्यक्षं न ह वा एवंविदि किंचनानन्नं भवतीति ॥ ५.२.१ ॥

*sa hovāca kiṃ me'nnaṃ bhaviṣyatīti yatkiṃcididamā śvabhya ā śakunibhya iti hocustadvā etadanasyānnamano ha vai nāma pratyakṣaṃ na ha vā evaṃvidi kiṃcanānannaṃ bhavatīti || 5.2.1 ||*

Prāṇa then asked, "What will my food be?" The other organs answered, "Whatever even dogs, birds and other animals can eat in this world is your food." All of that is food for ana, and Ana is a name of prāṇa. To someone who knows this, nothing is inedible. [That is, he can eat any food an animal can eat.]

### 5.2.2

स होवाच किं मे वासो भविष्यतीत्याप इति होचुस्तस्माद्वा एतदशिष्यन्तः पुरस्ताच्चोपरिष्टाच्चाद्भिः परिदधति लम्भुको ह वासो भवत्यनग्नो ह भवति ॥ ५.२.२ ॥

*sa hovāca kiṃ me vāso bhaviṣyatītyāpa iti hocustasmādvā etadaśiṣyantaḥ purastāccopariṣṭāccādbhiḥ paridadhati lambhuko ha vāso bhavatyanagno ha bhavati || 5.2.2 ||*

Prāṇa asked, "What will my covering be?" The organs answered, "Water." That is why people cover him with water before and after meals [by sipping water]. He is then clothed and no longer naked.

### 5.2.3

तद्धैतत्सत्यकामो जाबालो गोश्रुतये वैयाघ्रपद्यायोक्त्वोवाच यद्यप्येनच्छुष्काय स्थाणवे ब्रूयाज्जायेरन्नेवास्मिञ्छाखाः प्ररोहेयुः पलाशानीति ॥ ५.२.३ ॥

*taddhaitatsatyakāmo jābālo gośrutaye vaiyāghrapadyāyoktvovāca yadyapyenacchuṣkāya sthāṇave brūyājjāyerannevāsmiñchākhāḥ praroheyuḥ palāśānīti || 5.2.3 ||*

After telling this to Gośruti, the son of Vyāghrapada, Satyakāma Jābāla said, "If someone tells this even to a dry tree stump, branches and leaves will sprout from it."

### 5.2.4

अथ यदि महज्जिगमिषेदमावास्यायां दीक्षित्वा पौर्णमास्यां रात्रौ सर्वौषधस्य मन्थं दधिमधुनोरुपमथ्य ज्येष्ठाय श्रेष्ठाय स्वाहेत्यग्नावाज्यस्य हुत्वा मन्थे सम्पातमवनयेत् ॥ ५.२.४ ॥

*atha yadi mahajjigamiṣedamāvāsyāyāṃ dīkṣitvā paurṇamāsyāṃ rātrau sarvauṣadhasya manthaṃ dadhimadhunorupamathya jyeṣṭhāya śreṣṭhāya svāhetyagnāvājyasya hutvā manthe sampātamavanayet || 5.2.4 ||*

Now, anyone who wishes to attain greatness should first be initiated on a new moon day. Then, on the night of a full moon, he should prepare a paste of various herbs and blend it with curd and honey. He should offer this oblation into the fire, saying, "Jyeṣṭhāya śreṣṭhāya svāhā" [Svāhā to the oldest and to the best]. Whatever remains in the offering spoon he should put into the homa pot.

### 5.2.5

वसिष्ठाय स्वाहेत्यग्नावाज्यस्य हुत्वा मन्थे सम्पातमवनयेत्प्रतिष्ठायै स्वाहेत्यग्नावाज्यस्य हुत्वा मन्थे सम्पातमवनयेत्सम्पदे स्वाहेत्यग्नावाज्यस्य हुत्वा मन्थे सम्पातमवनयेदायतनाय स्वाहेत्यग्नावाज्यस्य हुत्वा मन्थे सम्पातमवनयेत् ॥ ५.२.५ ॥

*vasiṣṭhāya svāhetyagnāvājyasya hutvā manthe sampātamavanayetpratiṣṭhāyai svāhetyagnāvājyasya hutvā manthe sampātamavanayetsampade svāhetyagnāvājyasya hutvā manthe sampātamavanayedāyatanāya svāhetyagnāvājyasya hutvā manthe sampātamavanayet || 5.2.5 ||*

Saying "Vasiṣṭhāya svāhā" [svāhā to high standing], one should offer the oblation into the fire and put what remains in the offering spoon into the homa pot. Saying "Pratiṣṭhāyai svāhā" [svāhā to the support], one should do the same. Saying "Sampade svāhā" [svāhā to affluence], one should do the same. Saying "Āyatanāya svāhā" [svāhā to the abode], one should again offer the oblation into the fire and put what remains in the offering spoon into the homa pot.

### 5.2.6

अथ प्रतिसृप्याञ्जलौ मन्थमाधाय जपत्यमो नामास्यमा हि ते सर्वमिदं स हि ज्येष्ठः श्रेष्ठो राजाधिपतिः स मा ज्यैष्ठ्यं श्रैष्ठ्यं राज्यमाधिपत्यं गमयत्वहमेवेदं सर्वमसानीति ॥ ५.२.६ ॥

*atha pratisṛpyāñjalau manthamādhāya japatyamo nāmāsyamā hi te sarvamidaṃ sa hi jyeṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭho rājādhipatiḥ sa mā jyaiṣṭhyaṃ śraiṣṭhyaṃ rājyamādhipatyaṃ gamayatvahamevedaṃ sarvamasānīti || 5.2.6 ||*

Next, stepping a little way back from the fire with the homa pot in his hands, he repeats this mantra over and over: "You are named ama, because all this rests on you. You are the first, the best, outstanding and supreme. May I too be the first, the best, outstanding and supreme. May I be all this."

### 5.2.7

अथ खल्वेतयर्चा पच्छ आचामति तत्सवितुर्वृणीमह इत्याचामति वयं देवस्य भोजनमित्याचामति श्रेष्ठं सर्वधातममित्याचामति तुरं भगस्य धीमहीति सर्वं पिबति निर्णिज्य कंसं चमसं वा पश्चादग्नेः संविशति चर्मणि वा स्थण्डिले वा वाचंयमोऽप्रसाहः स यदि स्त्रियं पश्येत्समृद्धं कर्मेति विद्यात् ॥ ५.२.७ ॥

*atha khalvetayarcā paccha ācāmati tatsaviturvṛṇīmaha ityācāmati vayaṃ devasya bhojanamityācāmati śreṣṭhaṃ sarvadhātamamityācāmati turaṃ bhagasya dhīmahīti sarvaṃ pibati nirṇijya kaṃsaṃ camasaṃ vā paścādagneḥ saṃviśati carmaṇi vā sthaṇḍile vā vācaṃyamo'prasāhaḥ sa yadi striyaṃ paśyetsamṛddhaṃ karmeti vidyāt || 5.2.7 ||*

Then he recites this Ṛk mantra one foot at a time, eating some of what is in the homa pot with each part. He says, "We pray for that food of the shining deity," and eats a little. He says, "We eat the food of that deity," and eats a little. He says, "It is the best and the support of all," and eats a little. He says, "We quickly meditate on Bhaga," then eats the rest and washes the vessel or spoon. After that, with speech and mind under control, he lies down behind the fire, either on an animal skin or directly on the sacrificial ground. If a woman appears in his dream, he knows the rite has succeeded [and that he will succeed in whatever he does].

### 5.2.8

तदेष श्लोको यदा कर्मसु काम्येषु स्त्रियं स्वप्नेषु पश्यन्ति समृद्धिं तत्र जानीयात्तस्मिन्स्वप्ननिदर्शने तस्मिन्स्वप्ननिदर्शने ॥ ५.२.८ ॥ ॥ इति द्वितीयः खण्डः ॥

*tadeṣa śloko yadā karmasu kāmyeṣu striyaṃ svapneṣu paśyanti samṛddhiṃ tatra jānīyāttasminsvapnanidarśane tasminsvapnanidarśane || 5.2.8 || || iti dvitīyaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

A verse on this subject says: If you see a woman in a dream while performing a rite to fulfill a desire, the rite has succeeded. The dream lets you know this.

### 5.3.1

श्वेतकेतुर्हारुणेयः पञ्चालानां समितिमेयाय तं ह प्रवाहणो जैवलिरुवाच कुमारानु त्वाशिषत्पितेत्यनु हि भगव इति ॥ ५.३.१ ॥

*śvetaketurhāruṇeyaḥ pañcālānāṃ samitimeyāya taṃ ha pravāhaṇo jaivaliruvāca kumārānu tvāśiṣatpitetyanu hi bhagava iti || 5.3.1 ||*

Śvetaketu, the grandson of Aruṇa, once went to the court of the Pañcālas. Pravāhaṇa, the son of Jīvala, asked him, "Young man, has your father taught you?" [Śvetaketu answered,] "Yes, revered sir, he has."

### 5.3.2

वेत्थ यदितोऽधि प्रजाः प्रयन्तीति न भगव इति वेत्थ यथा पुनरावर्तन्त३ इति न भगव इति वेत्थ पथोर्देवयानस्य पितृयाणस्य च व्यावर्तना३ इति न भगव इति ॥ ५.३.२ ॥

*vettha yadito'dhi prajāḥ prayantīti na bhagava iti vettha yathā punarāvartanta3 iti na bhagava iti vettha pathordevayānasya pitṛyāṇasya ca vyāvartanā3 iti na bhagava iti || 5.3.2 ||*

[Pravāhaṇa asked,] "Do you have any idea where human beings go from this world in heaven?" "No, sir, I have no idea," [answered Śvetaketu]. "Do you know how they come back?" "No, sir, I do not." "Do you have any idea where the two paths, the path of the gods and the path of the ancestors, separate?" "No, sir, I do not know."

### 5.3.3

वेत्थ यथासौ लोको न सम्पूर्यत३ इति न भगव इति वेत्थ यथा पञ्चम्यामाहुतावापः पुरुषवचसो भवन्तीति नैव भगव इति ॥ ५.३.३ ॥

*vettha yathāsau loko na sampūryata3 iti na bhagava iti vettha yathā pañcamyāmāhutāvāpaḥ puruṣavacaso bhavantīti naiva bhagava iti || 5.3.3 ||*

[Pravāhaṇa asked,] "Do you know why the other world [the world of the moon] does not fill up with people?" [Śvetaketu answered,] "No, revered sir, I do not know." "Do you know why water comes to be called 'puruṣa' [man] after the fifth oblation?" "No, sir, I do not know."

### 5.3.4

अथानु किमनुशिष्ठोऽवोचथा यो हीमानि न विद्यात्कथं सोऽनुशिष्टो ब्रुवीतेति स हायस्तः पितुरर्धमेयाय तं होवाचाननुशिष्य वाव किल मा भगवानब्रवीदनु त्वाशिषमिति ॥ ५.३.४ ॥

*athānu kimanuśiṣṭho'vocathā yo hīmāni na vidyātkathaṃ so'nuśiṣṭo bruvīteti sa hāyastaḥ piturardhameyāya taṃ hovācānanuśiṣya vāva kila mā bhagavānabravīdanu tvāśiṣamiti || 5.3.4 ||*

[Pravāhaṇa said,] "Then why did you claim, 'I have been taught'? How can someone ignorant of these things say, 'I have been taught'?" Hurt by this, Śvetaketu returned to his father and said, "You told me, 'I have taught you,' but you have not truly taught me."

### 5.3.5

पञ्च मा राजन्यबन्धुः प्रश्नानप्राक्षीत्तेषां नैकंचनाशकं विवक्तुमिति स होवाच यथा मा त्वं तदैतानवदो यथाहमेषां नैकंचन वेद यद्यहमिमानवेदिष्यं कथं ते नावक्ष्यमिति ॥ ५.३.५ ॥

*pañca mā rājanyabandhuḥ praśnānaprākṣītteṣāṃ naikaṃcanāśakaṃ vivaktumiti sa hovāca yathā mā tvaṃ tadaitānavado yathāhameṣāṃ naikaṃcana veda yadyahamimānavediṣyaṃ kathaṃ te nāvakṣyamiti || 5.3.5 ||*

[Śvetaketu said,] "That friend of the princes asked me five questions, and I could not answer one of them." [He then told his father the five questions. After thinking them over for a while, his father] said, "As for the questions you brought back from the court, I cannot answer even one. If I had known the answers, why would I not have told you?"

### 5.3.6

स ह गौतमो राज्ञोऽर्धमेयाय तस्मै ह प्राप्तायार्हां चकार स ह प्रातः सभाग उदेयाय तं होवाच मानुषस्य भगवन्गौतम वित्तस्य वरं वृणीथा इति स होवाच तवैव राजन्मानुषं वित्तं यामेव कुमारस्यान्ते वाचमभाषथास्तामेव मे ब्रूहीति स ह कृच्छ्री बभूव ॥ ५.३.६ ॥

*sa ha gautamo rājño'rdhameyāya tasmai ha prāptāyārhāṃ cakāra sa ha prātaḥ sabhāga udeyāya taṃ hovāca mānuṣasya bhagavangautama vittasya varaṃ vṛṇīthā iti sa hovāca tavaiva rājanmānuṣaṃ vittaṃ yāmeva kumārasyānte vācamabhāṣathāstāmeva me brūhīti sa ha kṛcchrī babhūva || 5.3.6 ||*

Gautama then went to the king's palace, where the king received him with respect. The following morning, while the king was in his court, Gautama went to meet him. The king said, "Revered Gautama, ask me for a boon, anything a person might wish for." Gautama answered, "Keep those things. Please tell me what you said to my son." At this the king turned pale.

### 5.3.7

तं ह चिरं वसेत्याज्ञापयांचकार तं होवाच यथा मा त्वं गौतमावदो यथेयं न प्राक्त्वत्तः पुरा विद्या ब्राह्मणान्गच्छति तस्मादु सर्वेषु लोकेषु क्षत्रस्यैव प्रशासनमभूदिति तस्मै होवाच ॥ ५.३.७ ॥

*taṃ ha ciraṃ vasetyājñāpayāṃcakāra taṃ hovāca yathā mā tvaṃ gautamāvado yatheyaṃ na prāktvattaḥ purā vidyā brāhmaṇāngacchati tasmādu sarveṣu lokeṣu kṣatrasyaiva praśāsanamabhūditi tasmai hovāca || 5.3.7 ||*

The king then ordered Gautama to stay with him for a long time [as a brahmacari, and Gautama did so. One day] the king said to him, "Gautama, as for what you asked about, no brāhmin before you has had access to this knowledge. That is why, in the past, in all the worlds, the right to impart it belonged to the kṣatriyas alone." After saying this, he began to teach Gautama.

### 5.4.1

इति तृतीयः खण्डः ॥ असौ वाव लोको गौतमाग्निस्तस्यादित्य एव समिद्रश्मयो धूमोऽहरर्चिश्चन्द्रमा अङ्गारा नक्षत्राणि विस्फुलिङ्गाः ॥ ५.४.१ ॥

*iti tṛtīyaḥ khaṇḍaḥ || asau vāva loko gautamāgnistasyāditya eva samidraśmayo dhūmo'hararciścandramā aṅgārā nakṣatrāṇi visphuliṅgāḥ || 5.4.1 ||*

Gautama, heaven is the sacrificial fire. The sun is its fuel, the rays its smoke, the day its flame, the moon its embers and the stars its sparks.

### 5.4.2

तस्मिन्नेतस्मिन्नग्नौ देवाः श्रद्धां जुह्वति तस्या अहुतेः सोमो राजा संभवति ॥ ५.४.२ ॥ ॥ इति चतुर्थः खण्डः ॥

*tasminnetasminnagnau devāḥ śraddhāṃ juhvati tasyā ahuteḥ somo rājā saṃbhavati || 5.4.2 || || iti caturthaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

The gods [the organs of the sacrificer] offer water in respect to the fire [heaven]. From that oblation King Soma [the shining moon] appears.

### 5.5.1

पर्जन्यो वाव गौतमाग्निस्तस्य वायुरेव समिदभ्रं धूमो विद्युदर्चिरशनिरङ्गाराह्रादनयो विस्फुलिङ्गाः ॥ ५.५.१ ॥

*parjanyo vāva gautamāgnistasya vāyureva samidabhraṃ dhūmo vidyudarciraśaniraṅgārāhrādanayo visphuliṅgāḥ || 5.5.1 ||*

Gautama, the god of rain is the sacrificial fire. Air is its fuel, the cloud its smoke, lightning its flame, the thunderbolt its embers and thunder its sparks.

### 5.5.2

तस्मिन्नेतस्मिन्नग्नौ देवाः सोमं राजानं जुह्वति तस्या आहुतेर्वर्षं संभवति ॥ ५.५.२ ॥ ॥ इति पञ्चमः खण्डः ॥

*tasminnetasminnagnau devāḥ somaṃ rājānaṃ juhvati tasyā āhutervarṣaṃ saṃbhavati || 5.5.2 || || iti pañcamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

The gods offer King Soma [the shining moon] as the oblation into this fire. From that oblation rain appears.

### 5.6.1

पृथिवी वाव गौतमाग्निस्तस्याः संवत्सर एव समिदाकाशो धूमो रात्रिरर्चिर्दिशोऽङ्गारा अवान्तरदिशो विस्फुलिङ्गाः ॥ ५.६.१ ॥

*pṛthivī vāva gautamāgnistasyāḥ saṃvatsara eva samidākāśo dhūmo rātrirarcirdiśo'ṅgārā avāntaradiśo visphuliṅgāḥ || 5.6.1 ||*

Gautama, the earth is the sacrificial fire. The year is its fuel, the sky its smoke, night its flame, the quarters its embers and the intermediate directions its sparks.

### 5.6.2

तस्मिन्नेतस्मिन्नग्नौ देवा वर्षं जुह्वति तस्या आहुतेरन्नं संभवति ॥ ५.६.२ ॥ ॥ इति षष्ठः खण्डः ॥

*tasminnetasminnagnau devā varṣaṃ juhvati tasyā āhuterannaṃ saṃbhavati || 5.6.2 || || iti ṣaṣṭhaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

The gods offer rain as the oblation into this fire. From that oblation food appears.

### 5.7.1

पुरुषो वाव गौतमाग्निस्तस्य वागेव समित्प्राणो धूमो जिह्वार्चिश्चक्षुरङ्गाराः श्रोत्रं विस्फुलिङ्गाः ॥ ५.७.१ ॥

*puruṣo vāva gautamāgnistasya vāgeva samitprāṇo dhūmo jihvārciścakṣuraṅgārāḥ śrotraṃ visphuliṅgāḥ || 5.7.1 ||*

Gautama, man is the sacrificial fire. Speech is his fuel, prāṇa his smoke, the tongue his flame, the eyes his embers and the ears his sparks.

### 5.7.2

तस्मिन्नेतस्मिन्नग्नौ देवा अन्नं जुह्वति तस्या आहुते रेतः सम्भवति ॥ ५.७.२ ॥ ॥ इति सपतमः खण्डः ॥

*tasminnetasminnagnau devā annaṃ juhvati tasyā āhute retaḥ sambhavati || 5.7.2 || || iti sapatamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

The gods offer food as the oblation into this fire. From that oblation semen appears.

### 5.8.1

योषा वाव गौतमाग्निस्तस्या उपस्थ एव समिद्यदुपमन्त्रयते स धूमो योनिरर्चिर्यदन्तः करोति तेऽङ्गारा अभिनन्दा विस्फुलिङ्गाः ॥ ५.८.१ ॥ तस्मिन्नेतस्मिन्नग्नौ देवा रेतो जुह्वति तस्या आहुतेर्गर्भः संभवति ॥ ५.८.२ ॥ ॥ इति अष्टमः खण्डः ॥

*yoṣā vāva gautamāgnistasyā upastha eva samidyadupamantrayate sa dhūmo yonirarciryadantaḥ karoti te'ṅgārā abhinandā visphuliṅgāḥ || 5.8.1 || tasminnetasminnagnau devā reto juhvati tasyā āhutergarbhaḥ saṃbhavati || 5.8.2 || || iti aṣṭamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

Gautama, woman truly is the fire. Her sexual organ is the kindling wood (samidh); the invitation is the smoke; the vulva is the flame (arciḥ); the act within is the embers (aṅgārāḥ);
the parts giving pleasure are the sparks (visphuliṅgāḥ).
5.8.2 The gods offer the seed into this fire as an oblation, and from that oblation the embryo comes to be.

### 5.9.1

इति तु पञ्चम्यामाहुतावापः पुरुषवचसो भवन्तीति स उल्बावृतो गर्भो दश वा नव वा मासानन्तः शयित्वा यावद्वाथ जायते ॥ ५.९.१ ॥

*iti tu pañcamyāmāhutāvāpaḥ puruṣavacaso bhavantīti sa ulbāvṛto garbho daśa vā nava vā māsānantaḥ śayitvā yāvadvātha jāyate || 5.9.1 ||*

In this way, after the fifth oblation, water comes to be known as "man." Wrapped in membrane, the fetus lies in the mother's womb for about nine or ten months, and then it is born.

### 5.9.2

स जातो यावदायुषं जीवति तं प्रेतं दिष्टमितोऽग्नय एव हरन्ति यत एवेतो यतः संभूतो भवति ॥ ५.९.२ ॥ ॥ इति नवमः खण्डः ॥

*sa jāto yāvadāyuṣaṃ jīvati taṃ pretaṃ diṣṭamito'gnaya eva haranti yata eveto yataḥ saṃbhūto bhavati || 5.9.2 || || iti navamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

Once born, a person lives for as long as he is destined to. When he dies as ordained, his sons or disciples carry him from his home to the fire from which he came, the same fire from which he was born [and to which he owes his birth].

### 5.10.1

तद्य इत्थं विदुः। ये चेमेऽरण्ये श्रद्धा तप इत्युपासते तेऽर्चिषमभिसंभवन्त्यर्चिषोऽहरह्न आपूर्यमाणपक्षमापूर्यमाणपक्षाद्यान्षडुदङ्ङेति मासांस्तान् ॥ ५.१०.१ ॥ मासेभ्यः संवत्सरं संवत्सरादादित्यमादित्याच्चन्द्रमसं चन्द्रमसो विद्युतं तत्पुरुषोऽमानवः स एनान्ब्रह्म गमयत्येष देवयानः पन्था इति ॥ ५.१०.२ ॥

*tadya itthaṃ viduḥ| ye ceme'raṇye śraddhā tapa ityupāsate te'rciṣamabhisaṃbhavantyarciṣo'harahna āpūryamāṇapakṣamāpūryamāṇapakṣādyānṣaḍudaṅṅeti māsāṃstān || 5.10.1 || māsebhyaḥ saṃvatsaraṃ saṃvatsarādādityamādityāccandramasaṃ candramaso vidyutaṃ tatpuruṣo'mānavaḥ sa enānbrahma gamayatyeṣa devayānaḥ panthā iti || 5.10.2 ||*

People who know this [about the five fires], and people who live in the forest practicing austerities with faith, go after death to the world of light. From there they pass to the world of day, then to the world of the bright fortnight, then to the six months when the sun moves north. From there they go to the year, from the year to the sun, from the sun to the moon, and from the moon to lightning. There a being who is not human meets them and leads them to brahmaloka. This is the path of the gods.

### 5.10.3

अथ य इमे ग्राम इष्टापूर्ते दत्तमित्युपासते ते धूममभिसंभवन्ति धूमाद्रात्रिं रात्रेरपरपक्षमपरपक्षाद्यान्षड्दक्षिणैति मासांस्तान्नैते संवत्सरमभिप्राप्नुवन्ति ॥ ५.१०.३ ॥

*atha ya ime grāma iṣṭāpūrte dattamityupāsate te dhūmamabhisaṃbhavanti dhūmādrātriṃ rātreraparapakṣamaparapakṣādyānṣaḍdakṣiṇaiti māsāṃstānnaite saṃvatsaramabhiprāpnuvanti || 5.10.3 ||*

By contrast, people who live in the village and do public service, charity and similar works reach the world of smoke. From there they go to the world of night, from night to the world of the dark fortnight, and from the dark fortnight to the world of the six months when the sun moves south. So they never reach the world of the year.

### 5.10.4

मासेभ्यः पितृलोकं पितृलोकादाकाशमाकाशाच्चन्द्रमसमेष सोमो राजा तद्देवानामन्नं तं देवा भक्षयन्ति ॥ ५.१०.४ ॥

*māsebhyaḥ pitṛlokaṃ pitṛlokādākāśamākāśāccandramasameṣa somo rājā taddevānāmannaṃ taṃ devā bhakṣayanti || 5.10.4 ||*

From the six months of the southern solstice they go to the world of the ancestors, and from there to the sky. From the sky they go to the moon. This is King Soma, the food of the gods, and the gods delight in eating it.

### 5.10.5

तस्मिन्यवात्सम्पातमुषित्वाथैतमेवाध्वानं पुनर्निवर्तन्ते यथेतमाकाशमाकाशाद्वायुं वायुर्भूत्वा धूमो भवति धूमो भूत्वाभ्रं भवति ॥ ५.१०.५ ॥

*tasminyavātsampātamuṣitvāthaitamevādhvānaṃ punarnivartante yathetamākāśamākāśādvāyuṃ vāyurbhūtvā dhūmo bhavati dhūmo bhūtvābhraṃ bhavati || 5.10.5 ||*

He remains in the world of the moon until the fruits of his work run out, and then he comes back to this world by the way he went. First he goes to the sky and then to air. Having become air, he becomes smoke, and having become smoke, he becomes mist.

### 5.10.6

अभ्रं भूत्वा मेघो भवति मेघो भूत्वा प्रवर्षति त इह व्रीहियवा ओषधिवनस्पतयस्तिलमाषा इति जायन्तेऽतो वै खलु दुर्निष्प्रपतरं यो यो ह्यन्नमत्ति यो रेतः सिञ्चति तद्भूय एव भवति ॥ ५.१०.६ ॥

*abhraṃ bhūtvā megho bhavati megho bhūtvā pravarṣati ta iha vrīhiyavā oṣadhivanaspatayastilamāṣā iti jāyante'to vai khalu durniṣprapataraṃ yo yo hyannamatti yo retaḥ siñcati tadbhūya eva bhavati || 5.10.6 ||*

Having become mist, it turns into clouds. From clouds it becomes rain and falls to the earth. In the end it grows as paddy, barley, plants, trees, sesame, beans and so on. Moving on from this state is very hard. Those who eat these things produce children like themselves.

### 5.10.7

तद्य इह रमणीयचरणा अभ्याशो ह यत्ते रमणीयां योनिमापद्येरन्ब्राह्मणयोनिं वा क्षत्रिययोनिं वा वैश्ययोनिं वाथ य इह कपूयचरणा अभ्याशो ह यत्ते कपूयां योनिमापद्येरञ्श्वयोनिं वा सूकरयोनिं वा चण्डालयोनिं वा ॥ ५.१०.७ ॥

*tadya iha ramaṇīyacaraṇā abhyāśo ha yatte ramaṇīyāṃ yonimāpadyeranbrāhmaṇayoniṃ vā kṣatriyayoniṃ vā vaiśyayoniṃ vātha ya iha kapūyacaraṇā abhyāśo ha yatte kapūyāṃ yonimāpadyerañśvayoniṃ vā sūkarayoniṃ vā caṇḍālayoniṃ vā || 5.10.7 ||*

Among them, those who did good work in this world [in their past life] gain a good birth to match, being born as a brāhmin, a kṣatriya or a vaiśya. Those who did bad work in this world [in their past life] gain a bad birth to match, being born as a dog, a pig or a casteless person.

### 5.10.8

अथैतयोः पथोर्न कतरेणचन तानीमानि क्षुद्राण्यसकृदावर्तीनि भूतानि भवन्ति जायस्व म्रियस्वेत्येतत्तृतीयंस्थानं तेनासौ लोको न सम्पूर्यते तस्माज्जुगुप्सेत तदेष श्लोकः ॥ ५.१०.८ ॥

*athaitayoḥ pathorna katareṇacana tānīmāni kṣudrāṇyasakṛdāvartīni bhūtāni bhavanti jāyasva mriyasvetyetattṛtīyaṃsthānaṃ tenāsau loko na sampūryate tasmājjugupseta tadeṣa ślokaḥ || 5.10.8 ||*

Those who follow neither of these two paths are born again and again among small animals and insects. [Of those born into] this third state it can be said, "Be born and die." This is why the other world never fills up. So one should despise this state. Here is a verse on the subject:

### 5.10.9

स्तेनो हिरण्यस्य सुरां पिबंश्च गुरोस्तल्पमावसन्ब्रह्महा चैते पतन्ति चत्वारः पञ्चमश्चाचरंस्तैरिति ॥ ५.१०.९ ॥

*steno hiraṇyasya surāṃ pibaṃśca gurostalpamāvasanbrahmahā caite patanti catvāraḥ pañcamaścācaraṃstairiti || 5.10.9 ||*

Whoever steals gold, drinks liquor, sleeps with his teacher's wife or kills a brāhmin: these four are lost. Lost as well is the fifth, the one who associates with them.

### 5.10.10

अथ ह य एतानेवं पञ्चाग्नीन्वेद न सह तैरप्याचरन्पाप्मना लिप्यते शुद्धः पूतः पुण्यलोको भवति य एवं वेद य एवं वेद ॥ ५.१०.१० ॥ ॥ इति दशमः खण्डः ॥

*atha ha ya etānevaṃ pañcāgnīnveda na saha tairapyācaranpāpmanā lipyate śuddhaḥ pūtaḥ puṇyaloko bhavati ya evaṃ veda ya evaṃ veda || 5.10.10 || || iti daśamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

But the one who knows the five fires stays pure even in the company of such people. Whoever knows this is pure and innocent, and goes to a holy world after death.

### 5.11.1

प्राचीनशाल औपमन्यवः सत्ययज्ञः पौलुषिरिन्द्रद्युम्नो भाल्लवेयो जनः शार्कराक्ष्यो बुडिल आश्वतराश्विस्ते हैते महाशाला महाश्रोत्रियाः समेत्य मीमांसां चक्रुः को न आत्मा किं ब्रह्मेति ॥ ५.११.१ ॥

*prācīnaśāla aupamanyavaḥ satyayajñaḥ pauluṣirindradyumno bhāllaveyo janaḥ śārkarākṣyo buḍila āśvatarāśviste haite mahāśālā mahāśrotriyāḥ sametya mīmāṃsāṃ cakruḥ ko na ātmā kiṃ brahmeti || 5.11.1 ||*

Prācīnaśāla, the son of Upamanyu; Satyayajña, the son of Puluṣa; Indradyumna, the son of Bhāllavi; Jana, the son of Śarkarākṣa; and Buḍila, the son of Aśvatarāśva: these eminent householders and Vedic scholars once came together to settle a question. Who is our Self? And what is Brahman?

### 5.11.2

ते ह सम्पादयांचक्रुरुद्दालको वै भगवन्तोऽयमारुणिः सम्प्रतीममात्मानं वैश्वानरमध्येति तं हन्ताभ्यागच्छामेति तं हाभ्याजग्मुः ॥ ५.११.२ ॥

*te ha sampādayāṃcakruruddālako vai bhagavanto'yamāruṇiḥ sampratīmamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramadhyeti taṃ hantābhyāgacchāmeti taṃ hābhyājagmuḥ || 5.11.2 ||*

They talked it over and decided what to do. One of them said, "Revered sirs, Uddālaka Āruṇi is the one who now knows about this Vaiśvānara Self. Let us go to him." So all of them went to him.

### 5.11.3

स ह सम्पादयांचकार प्रक्ष्यन्ति मामिमे महाशाला महाश्रोत्रियास्तेभ्यो न सर्वमिव प्रतिपत्स्ये हन्ताहमन्यमभ्यनुशासानीति ॥ ५.११.३ ॥

*sa ha sampādayāṃcakāra prakṣyanti māmime mahāśālā mahāśrotriyāstebhyo na sarvamiva pratipatsye hantāhamanyamabhyanuśāsānīti || 5.11.3 ||*

[Uddālaka realized they had come to ask him about the Vaiśvānara Ātman.] He thought, "These eminent householders and Vedic scholars will question me, and I may be unable to answer everything. So I will send them to another teacher."

### 5.11.4

तान्होवाचाश्वपतिर्वै भगवन्तोऽयं कैकेयः सम्प्रतीममात्मानं वैश्वानरमध्येति तंहन्ताभ्यागच्छामेति तंहाभ्याजग्मुः ॥ ५.११.४ ॥

*tānhovācāśvapatirvai bhagavanto'yaṃ kaikeyaḥ sampratīmamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramadhyeti taṃhantābhyāgacchāmeti taṃhābhyājagmuḥ || 5.11.4 ||*

Uddālaka told them, "Sirs, at this time only King Aśvapati, the son of Kekaya, knows about the Vaiśvānara Ātman. With your permission, we will go to him." They then set off to see Aśvapati.

### 5.11.5

तेभ्यो ह प्राप्तेभ्यः पृथगर्हाणि कारयांचकार स ह प्रातः संजिहान उवाच न मे स्तेनो जनपदे न कर्दर्यो न मद्यपो नानाहिताग्निर्नाविद्वान्न स्वैरी स्वैरिणी कुतो यक्ष्यमाणो वै भगवन्तोऽहमस्मि यावदेकैकस्मा ऋत्विजे धनं दास्यामि तावद्भगवद्भ्यो दास्यामि वसन्तु भगवन्त इति ॥ ५.११.५ ॥

*tebhyo ha prāptebhyaḥ pṛthagarhāṇi kārayāṃcakāra sa ha prātaḥ saṃjihāna uvāca na me steno janapade na kardaryo na madyapo nānāhitāgnirnāvidvānna svairī svairiṇī kuto yakṣyamāṇo vai bhagavanto'hamasmi yāvadekaikasmā ṛtvije dhanaṃ dāsyāmi tāvadbhagavadbhyo dāsyāmi vasantu bhagavanta iti || 5.11.5 ||*

When they arrived, Aśvapati had each guest honored separately. The next morning, on rising from bed, he said to them, "In my state there is no thief, no miser, no drunkard, no brāhmin who fails to perform the agnihotra sacrifice, no uneducated person, no adulterer, and so no adulteress. Sirs, I am performing a sacrifice. I will give each of you the same amount of money I give to each priest in it. Revered sirs, please stay here."

### 5.11.6

ते होचुर्येन हैवार्थेन पुरुषश्चरेत्तंहैव वदेदात्मानमेवेमं वैश्वानरं सम्प्रत्यध्येषि तमेव नो ब्रूहीति ॥ ५.११.६ ॥

*te hocuryena haivārthena puruṣaścarettaṃhaiva vadedātmānamevemaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ sampratyadhyeṣi tameva no brūhīti || 5.11.6 ||*

They replied, "When someone pays a visit, the first thing he should do is say why he has come. You are the one who knows about the Vaiśvānara Ātman at this time. Please tell us about it."

### 5.11.7

तान्होवाच प्रातर्वः प्रतिवक्तास्मीति ते ह समित्पाणयः पूर्वाह्णे प्रतिचक्रमिरे तान्हानुपनीयैवैतदुवाच ॥ ५.११.७ ॥ ॥ इति एकादशः खण्डः ॥

*tānhovāca prātarvaḥ prativaktāsmīti te ha samitpāṇayaḥ pūrvāhṇe praticakramire tānhānupanīyaivaitaduvāca || 5.11.7 || || iti ekādaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

He said to them, "I will answer you tomorrow morning." The next day, before noon, they returned to him carrying fuel in their hands. Without initiating them, he said the following.

### 5.12.1

औपमन्यव कं त्वमात्मानमुपास्स इति दिवमेव भगवो राजन्निति होवाचैष वै सुतेजा आत्मा वैश्वानरो यं त्वमात्मानमुपास्से तस्मात्तव सुतं प्रसुतमासुतं कुले दृश्यते ॥ ५.१२.१ ॥

*aupamanyava kaṃ tvamātmānamupāssa iti divameva bhagavo rājanniti hovācaiṣa vai sutejā ātmā vaiśvānaro yaṃ tvamātmānamupāsse tasmāttava sutaṃ prasutamāsutaṃ kule dṛśyate || 5.12.1 ||*

The king said, "Son of Upamanyu, what do you worship as the Vaiśvānara Self?" The son of Upamanyu answered, "King, I worship heaven." The king said, "The Self you worship is the Vaiśvānara Self called Suteja, meaning bright and beautiful. That is why everyone born into your family is bright, brighter and brighter still."

### 5.12.2

अत्स्यन्नं पश्यसि प्रियमत्त्यन्नं पश्यति प्रियं भवत्यस्य ब्रह्मवर्चसं कुले य एतमेवमात्मानं वैश्वानरमुपास्ते मूधा त्वेष आत्मन इति होवाच मूर्धा ते व्यपतिष्यद्यन्मां नागमिष्य इति ॥ ५.१२.२ ॥ ॥ इति द्वादशः खण्डः ॥

*atsyannaṃ paśyasi priyamattyannaṃ paśyati priyaṃ bhavatyasya brahmavarcasaṃ kule ya etamevamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramupāste mūdhā tveṣa ātmana iti hovāca mūrdhā te vyapatiṣyadyanmāṃ nāgamiṣya iti || 5.12.2 || || iti dvādaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

"You enjoy your food and you see your loved ones and all that is pleasant. Whoever worships the Vaiśvānara Self in this way enjoys food and sees his loved ones. His family also has children who shine with the radiance of Brahman [they become known for good conduct and scholarship]. But heaven is like the head of the Self." The king then said, "Had you not come to me, your head would have fallen off."

### 5.13.1

अथ होवाच सत्ययज्ञं पौलुषिं प्राचीनयोग्य कं त्वमात्मानमुपास्स इत्यादित्यमेव भगवो राजन्निति होवाचैष वै विश्वरूप आत्मा वैश्वानरो यं त्वमात्मानमुपास्से तस्मात्तव बहु विश्वरूपं कुले दृश्यते ॥ ५.१३.१ ॥

*atha hovāca satyayajñaṃ pauluṣiṃ prācīnayogya kaṃ tvamātmānamupāssa ityādityameva bhagavo rājanniti hovācaiṣa vai viśvarūpa ātmā vaiśvānaro yaṃ tvamātmānamupāsse tasmāttava bahu viśvarūpaṃ kule dṛśyate || 5.13.1 ||*

The king then said to Satyayajña Pauluṣi, "Prācīnayogya, what do you worship as the Self?" Satyayajña answered, "I worship Āditya, the sun, revered king." The king said, "What you worship as the Vaiśvānara Ātman is called Viśvarūpa. That is why your family has wealth in many forms."

### 5.13.2

प्रवृत्तोऽश्वतरीरथो दासीनिष्कोऽत्स्यन्नं पश्यसि प्रियमत्त्यन्नं पश्यति प्रियं भवत्यस्य ब्रह्मवर्चसं कुले य एतमेवमात्मानं वैश्वानरमुपास्ते चक्षुषेतदात्मन इति होवाचान्धोऽभविष्यो यन्मां नागमिष्य इति ॥ ५.१३.२ ॥ ॥ इति त्रयोदशः खण्डः ॥

*pravṛtto'śvatarīratho dāsīniṣko'tsyannaṃ paśyasi priyamattyannaṃ paśyati priyaṃ bhavatyasya brahmavarcasaṃ kule ya etamevamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramupāste cakṣuṣetadātmana iti hovācāndho'bhaviṣyo yanmāṃ nāgamiṣya iti || 5.13.2 || || iti trayodaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

"You now have chariots drawn by mules, maidservants who wait on you, and a necklace. You enjoy your food and you see your loved ones and all that is pleasant. Whoever worships the Vaiśvānara Self in this way enjoys food and sees his loved ones. His family also has children who shine with the radiance of Brahman [they become known for good conduct and scholarship]. But this Āditya is like the eye of the Self." The king then said, "Had you not come to me, you would have gone blind."

### 5.14.1

अथ होवाचेन्द्रद्युम्नं भाल्लवेयं वैयाघ्रपद्य कं त्वमात्मानमुपास्स इति वायुमेव भगवो राजन्निति होवाचैष वै पृथग्वर्त्मात्मा वैश्वानरो यं त्वमात्मानमुपास्से तस्मात्त्वां पृथग्बलय आयन्ति पृथग्रथश्रेणयोऽनुयन्ति ॥ ५.१४.१ ॥

*atha hovācendradyumnaṃ bhāllaveyaṃ vaiyāghrapadya kaṃ tvamātmānamupāssa iti vāyumeva bhagavo rājanniti hovācaiṣa vai pṛthagvartmātmā vaiśvānaro yaṃ tvamātmānamupāsse tasmāttvāṃ pṛthagbalaya āyanti pṛthagrathaśreṇayo'nuyanti || 5.14.1 ||*

The king then said to Indradyumna Bhāllaveya, "Vaiyāghrapadya, what do you worship as the Self?" Indradyumna answered, "I worship Vāyu [air], revered king." The king said, "What you worship as the Vaiśvānara Ātman is called Pṛthagvartmā [one who changes direction]. That is why gifts reach you from every direction, and why different kinds of chariots follow you in rows."

### 5.14.2

अत्स्यन्नं पश्यसि प्रियमत्त्यन्नं पश्यति प्रियं भवत्यस्य ब्रह्मवर्चसं कुले य एतमेवमात्मानं वैश्वानरमुपास्ते प्राणस्त्वेष आत्मन इति होवाच प्राणस्त उदक्रमिष्यद्यन्मां नागमिष्य इति ॥ ५.१४.२ ॥ ॥ इति चतुर्दशः खण्डः ॥

*atsyannaṃ paśyasi priyamattyannaṃ paśyati priyaṃ bhavatyasya brahmavarcasaṃ kule ya etamevamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramupāste prāṇastveṣa ātmana iti hovāca prāṇasta udakramiṣyadyanmāṃ nāgamiṣya iti || 5.14.2 || || iti caturdaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

"You enjoy your food and you see your loved ones and all that is pleasant. Whoever worships the Vaiśvānara Self in this way enjoys food and sees his loved ones. His family also has children who shine with the radiance of Brahman [they become known for good conduct and scholarship]. But Vāyu [air] is like the life [the vital force] of the Self." The king then said, "Had you not come to me, your vital force would have left your body."

### 5.15.1

अथ होवाच जनंशार्कराक्ष्य कं त्वमात्मानमुपास्स इत्याकाशमेव भगवो राजन्निति होवाचैष वै बहुल आत्मा वैश्वानरो यं त्वमात्मानमुपस्से तस्मात्त्वं बहुलोऽसि प्रजया च धनेन च ॥ ५.१५.१ ॥

*atha hovāca janaṃśārkarākṣya kaṃ tvamātmānamupāssa ityākāśameva bhagavo rājanniti hovācaiṣa vai bahula ātmā vaiśvānaro yaṃ tvamātmānamupasse tasmāttvaṃ bahulo'si prajayā ca dhanena ca || 5.15.1 ||*

The king then said to Jana, "Sārkarākṣya, what do you worship as the Self?" Jana answered, "I worship Ākāśa [space], revered king." The king said, "What you worship as the Vaiśvānara Ātman is called Bahula [pervasive]. That is why you have so many children and so much wealth."

### 5.15.2

अत्स्यन्नं पश्यसि प्रियमत्त्यन्नं पश्यति प्रियं भवत्यस्य ब्रह्मवर्चसं कुले य एतमेवमात्मानं वैश्वानरमुपास्ते संदेहस्त्वेष आत्मन इति होवाच संदेहस्ते व्यशीर्यद्यन्मां नागमिष्य इति ॥ ५.१५.२ ॥ ॥ इति पञ्चदशः खण्डः ॥

*atsyannaṃ paśyasi priyamattyannaṃ paśyati priyaṃ bhavatyasya brahmavarcasaṃ kule ya etamevamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramupāste saṃdehastveṣa ātmana iti hovāca saṃdehaste vyaśīryadyanmāṃ nāgamiṣya iti || 5.15.2 || || iti pañcadaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

"You enjoy your food and you see your loved ones and all that is pleasant. Whoever worships the Vaiśvānara Self in this way enjoys food and sees his loved ones. His family also has children who shine with the radiance of Brahman [they become known for good conduct and scholarship]. But Ākāśa [space] is like the middle part of the Self." The king then said, "Had you not come to me, the middle part of your body would have shrunk."

### 5.16.1

अथ होवाच बुडिलमाश्वतराश्विं वैयाघ्रपद्य कं त्वमात्मानमुपास्स इत्यप एव भगवो राजन्निति होवाचैष वै रयिरात्मा वैश्वानरो यं त्वमात्मानमुपास्से तस्मात्त्वंरयिमान्पुष्टिमानसि ॥ ५.१६.१ ॥

*atha hovāca buḍilamāśvatarāśviṃ vaiyāghrapadya kaṃ tvamātmānamupāssa ityapa eva bhagavo rājanniti hovācaiṣa vai rayirātmā vaiśvānaro yaṃ tvamātmānamupāsse tasmāttvaṃrayimānpuṣṭimānasi || 5.16.1 ||*

Next the king asked Buḍila Āśvatarāśvi, "O Vaiyāghrapadya, what do you worship as the Self?" "Water, revered king," Buḍila answered. The king said, "What you worship as the Vaiśvānara Ātman goes by the name Rayi [wealth]. That is the reason you are rich and healthy."

### 5.16.2

अत्स्यन्नं पश्यसि प्रियमत्त्यन्नं पश्यति प्रियं भवत्यस्य ब्रह्मवर्चसं कुले य एतमेवमात्मानं वैश्वानरमुपास्ते बस्तिस्त्वेष आत्मन इति होवाच बस्तिस्ते व्यभेत्स्यद्यन्मां नागमिष्य इति ॥ ५.१६.२ ॥ ॥ इति षोडशः खण्डः ॥

*atsyannaṃ paśyasi priyamattyannaṃ paśyati priyaṃ bhavatyasya brahmavarcasaṃ kule ya etamevamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramupāste bastistveṣa ātmana iti hovāca bastiste vyabhetsyadyanmāṃ nāgamiṣya iti || 5.16.2 || || iti ṣoḍaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

"You take pleasure in eating, and you see your loved ones and all that is pleasant. Whoever worships the Vaiśvānara Self in this way enjoys food and gets to see his loved ones. His family also has children who shine with the radiance of Brahman [that is, they become known for their good conduct and scholarship]. But water is like the Self's bladder." The king then added, "Had you not come to me, your bladder would have burst."

### 5.17.1

अथ होवाचोद्दालकमारुणिं गौतम कं त्वमात्मानमुपस्स इति पृथिवीमेव भगवो राजन्निति होवाचैष वै प्रतिष्ठात्मा वैश्वानरो यं त्वमात्मानमुपास्से तस्मात्त्वं प्रतिष्ठितोऽसि प्रजया च पशुभिश्च ॥ ५.१७.१ ॥

*atha hovācoddālakamāruṇiṃ gautama kaṃ tvamātmānamupassa iti pṛthivīmeva bhagavo rājanniti hovācaiṣa vai pratiṣṭhātmā vaiśvānaro yaṃ tvamātmānamupāsse tasmāttvaṃ pratiṣṭhito'si prajayā ca paśubhiśca || 5.17.1 ||*

Next the king asked Uddālaka Āruṇi, "O Gautama, what do you worship as the Self?" "The earth, revered king," Uddālaka answered. The king said, "What you worship as the Vaiśvānara Ātman goes by the name Pratiṣṭhā [the support]. That is the reason you have so many children and animals to support you."

### 5.17.2

अत्स्यन्नं पश्यसि प्रियमत्त्यन्नं पश्यति प्रियं भवत्यस्य ब्रह्मवर्चसं कुले य एतमेवमात्मानं वैश्वानरमुपास्ते पादौ त्वेतावात्मन इति होवाच पादौ ते व्यम्लास्येतां यन्मां नागमिष्य इति ॥ ५.१७.२ ॥ ॥ इति सप्तदशः खण्डः ॥

*atsyannaṃ paśyasi priyamattyannaṃ paśyati priyaṃ bhavatyasya brahmavarcasaṃ kule ya etamevamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramupāste pādau tvetāvātmana iti hovāca pādau te vyamlāsyetāṃ yanmāṃ nāgamiṣya iti || 5.17.2 || || iti saptadaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

"You take pleasure in eating, and you see your loved ones and all that is pleasant. Whoever worships the Vaiśvānara Self in this way enjoys food and gets to see his loved ones. His family also has children who shine with the radiance of Brahman [that is, they become known for their good conduct and scholarship]. But the earth is like the Self's feet." The king then added, "Had you not come to me, your feet would have grown extremely weak."

### 5.18.1

तान्होवाचैते वै खलु यूयं पृथगिवेममात्मानं वैश्वानरं विद्वांसोऽन्नमत्थ यस्त्वेतमेवं प्रादेशमात्रमभिविमानमात्मानं वैश्वानरमुपास्ते स सर्वेषु लोकेषु सर्वेषु भूतेषु सर्वेष्वात्मस्वन्नमत्ति ॥ ५.१८.१ ॥

*tānhovācaite vai khalu yūyaṃ pṛthagivemamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ vidvāṃso'nnamattha yastvetamevaṃ prādeśamātramabhivimānamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramupāste sa sarveṣu lokeṣu sarveṣu bhūteṣu sarveṣvātmasvannamatti || 5.18.1 ||*

The king said to the brāhmins: "All of you here meditate on only a part of the Vaiśvānara Self. [That is why, when you eat, you think you are eating separately.] The one who worships the Self as all-pervading and infinite enjoys food through whoever eats in the worlds, through all beings, and through all selves."

### 5.18.2

तस्य ह वा एतस्यात्मनो वैश्वानरस्य मूर्धैव सुतेजाश्चक्षुर्विश्वरूपः प्राणः पृथग्वर्त्मात्मा संदेहो बहुलो बस्तिरेव रयिः पृथिव्येव पादावुर एव वेदिर्लोमानि बर्हिर्हृदयं गार्हपत्यो मनोऽन्वाहार्यपचन आस्यमाहवनीयः ॥ ५.१८.२ ॥ ॥ इति अष्टादशः खण्डः ॥

*tasya ha vā etasyātmano vaiśvānarasya mūrdhaiva sutejāścakṣurviśvarūpaḥ prāṇaḥ pṛthagvartmātmā saṃdeho bahulo bastireva rayiḥ pṛthivyeva pādāvura eva vedirlomāni barhirhṛdayaṃ gārhapatyo mano'nvāhāryapacana āsyamāhavanīyaḥ || 5.18.2 || || iti aṣṭādaśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

The head of this Vaiśvānara Self is Suteja ["the bright and beautiful," heaven]. Its eye is Viśvarūpa ["having many forms," the sun]. Its prāṇa is Pṛthagvartmā ["one who changes direction," air]. Its middle part is Bahula ["pervasive," space]. Its bladder is Rayi ["wealth," water]. Its feet are the earth [Pratiṣṭhā, "the support"]. Its chest is the sacrificial altar, and the hair on its chest is the kuśa grass. Its heart is the Gārhapatya fire, its mind is the Anvāhāryapacana [the Dakṣiṇāgni] fire, and its mouth is the Āhavanīya fire.

### 5.19.1

तद्यद्भक्तं प्रथममागच्छेत्तद्धोमीयं स यां प्रथमामाहुतिं जुहुयात्तां जुहुयात्प्राणाय स्वाहेति प्राणस्तृप्यति ॥ ५.१९.१ ॥

*tadyadbhaktaṃ prathamamāgacchettaddhomīyaṃ sa yāṃ prathamāmāhutiṃ juhuyāttāṃ juhuyātprāṇāya svāheti prāṇastṛpyati || 5.19.1 ||*

The first portion of food is like the first oblation. The one eating should offer it as an oblation to prāṇa with the words "Prāṇāya svāhā" [I offer this as an oblation to prāṇa]. This pleases your prāṇa.

### 5.19.2

प्राणे तृप्यति चक्षुस्तृप्यति चक्षुषि तृप्यत्यादित्यस्तृप्यत्यादित्ये तृप्यति द्यौस्तृप्यति दिवि तृप्यन्त्यां यत्किंच द्यौश्चादित्यश्चाधितिष्ठतस्तत्तृप्यति तस्यानुतृप्तिं तृप्यति प्रजया पशुभिरन्नाद्येन तेजसा ब्रह्मवर्चसेनेति ॥ ५.१९.२ ॥ ॥ इति एकोनविंशः खण्डः ॥

*prāṇe tṛpyati cakṣustṛpyati cakṣuṣi tṛpyatyādityastṛpyatyāditye tṛpyati dyaustṛpyati divi tṛpyantyāṃ yatkiṃca dyauścādityaścādhitiṣṭhatastattṛpyati tasyānutṛptiṃ tṛpyati prajayā paśubhirannādyena tejasā brahmavarcaseneti || 5.19.2 || || iti ekonaviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

If prāṇa is pleased, the eye is pleased. If the eye is pleased, the sun is pleased. If the sun is pleased, heaven is pleased. If heaven is pleased, everything ruled by heaven and the sun is pleased. And once that is pleased, the eater finds joy in his children, his animals, plentiful food, bodily strength, and his good life and scholarship.

### 5.20.1

अथ यां द्वितीयां जुहुयात्तां जुहुयाद्व्यानाय स्वाहेति व्यानस्तृप्यति ॥ ५.२०.१ ॥

*atha yāṃ dvitīyāṃ juhuyāttāṃ juhuyādvyānāya svāheti vyānastṛpyati || 5.20.1 ||*

When he [the eater] makes the second oblation, he does so with the words "Vyānāya svāhā" [I offer this as an oblation to vyāna]. This pleases vyāna.

### 5.20.2

व्याने तृप्यति श्रोत्रं तृप्यति श्रोत्रे तृप्यति चन्द्रमास्तृप्यति चन्द्रमसि तृप्यति दिशस्तृप्यन्ति दिक्षु तृप्यन्तीषु यत्किंच दिशश्च चन्द्रमाश्चाधितिष्ठन्ति तत्तृप्यति तस्यानु तृप्तिं तृप्यति प्रजया पशुभिरन्नाद्येन तेजसा ब्रह्मवर्चसेनेति ॥ ५.२०.२ ॥ ॥ इति विंशः खण्डः ॥

*vyāne tṛpyati śrotraṃ tṛpyati śrotre tṛpyati candramāstṛpyati candramasi tṛpyati diśastṛpyanti dikṣu tṛpyantīṣu yatkiṃca diśaśca candramāścādhitiṣṭhanti tattṛpyati tasyānu tṛptiṃ tṛpyati prajayā paśubhirannādyena tejasā brahmavarcaseneti || 5.20.2 || || iti viṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

If vyāna is pleased, the ear is pleased. If the ear is pleased, the moon is pleased. If the moon is pleased, the quarters are pleased. If the quarters are pleased, everything ruled by the quarters and the moon is pleased. And once that is pleased, the eater finds joy in his children, his animals, plentiful food, bodily strength, and his good life and scholarship.

### 5.21.1

अथ यां तृतीयां जुहुयात्तां जुहुयादपानाय स्वाहेत्यपानस्तृप्यति ॥ ५.२१.१ ॥

*atha yāṃ tṛtīyāṃ juhuyāttāṃ juhuyādapānāya svāhetyapānastṛpyati || 5.21.1 ||*

When he [the eater] makes the third oblation, he does so with the words "Apānāya svāhā" [I offer this as an oblation to apāna]. This pleases apāna.

### 5.21.2

अपाने तृप्यति वाक्तृप्यति वाचि तृप्यन्त्यामग्निस्तृप्यत्यग्नौ तृप्यति पृथिवी तृप्यति पृथिव्यां तृप्यन्त्यां यत्किंच पृथिवी चाग्निश्चाधितिष्ठतस्तत्तृप्यति तस्यानु तृप्तिं तृप्यति प्रजया पशुभिरन्नाद्येन तेजसा ब्रह्मवर्चसेनेति ॥ ५.२१.२ ॥ ॥ इति एकविंशः खण्डः ॥

*apāne tṛpyati vāktṛpyati vāci tṛpyantyāmagnistṛpyatyagnau tṛpyati pṛthivī tṛpyati pṛthivyāṃ tṛpyantyāṃ yatkiṃca pṛthivī cāgniścādhitiṣṭhatastattṛpyati tasyānu tṛptiṃ tṛpyati prajayā paśubhirannādyena tejasā brahmavarcaseneti || 5.21.2 || || iti ekaviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

If apāna is pleased, the organ of speech is pleased. If the organ of speech is pleased, fire is pleased. If fire is pleased, the earth is pleased. If the earth is pleased, everything governed by the earth and fire is pleased. And once that is pleased, the eater finds joy in his children, his animals, plentiful food, bodily strength, and his good life and scholarship.

### 5.22.1

अथ यां चतुर्थीं जुहुयात्तां जुहुयात्समानाय स्वाहेति समानस्तृप्यति ॥ ५.२२.१ ॥

*atha yāṃ caturthīṃ juhuyāttāṃ juhuyātsamānāya svāheti samānastṛpyati || 5.22.1 ||*

When he [the eater] makes the fourth oblation, he does so with the words "Samānāya svāhā" [I offer this as an oblation to samāna]. This pleases samāna.

### 5.22.2

समाने तृप्यति मनस्तृप्यति मनसि तृप्यति पर्जन्यस्तृप्यति पर्जन्ये तृप्यति विद्युत्तृप्यति विद्युति तृप्यन्त्यां यत्किंच विद्युच्च पर्जन्यश्चाधितिष्ठतस्तत्तृप्यति तस्यानु तृप्तिं तृप्यति प्रजया पशुभिरन्नाद्येन तेजसा ब्रह्मवर्चसेनेति ॥ ५.२२.२ ॥ ॥ इति द्वाविंशः खण्डः ॥

*samāne tṛpyati manastṛpyati manasi tṛpyati parjanyastṛpyati parjanye tṛpyati vidyuttṛpyati vidyuti tṛpyantyāṃ yatkiṃca vidyucca parjanyaścādhitiṣṭhatastattṛpyati tasyānu tṛptiṃ tṛpyati prajayā paśubhirannādyena tejasā brahmavarcaseneti || 5.22.2 || || iti dvāviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

If samāna is pleased, the mind is pleased. If the mind is pleased, the cloud is pleased. If the cloud is pleased, lightning is pleased. If lightning is pleased, everything governed by lightning and the cloud is pleased. And once that is pleased, the eater finds joy in his children, his animals, plentiful food, bodily strength, and his good life and scholarship.

### 5.23.1

अथ यां पञ्चमीं जुहुयात्तां जुहुयादुदानाय स्वाहेत्युदानस्तृप्यति ॥ ५.२३.१ ॥

*atha yāṃ pañcamīṃ juhuyāttāṃ juhuyādudānāya svāhetyudānastṛpyati || 5.23.1 ||*

When he [the eater] makes the fifth oblation, he does so with the words "Udānāya svāhā" [I offer this as an oblation to udāna]. This pleases udāna.

### 5.23.2

उदाने तृप्यति त्वक्तृप्यति त्वचि तृप्यन्त्यां वायुस्तृप्यति वायौ तृप्यत्याकाशस्तृप्यत्याकाशे तृप्यति यत्किंच वायुश्चाकाशश्चाधितिष्ठतस्तत्तृप्यति तस्यानु तृप्तिं तृप्यति प्रजया पशुभिरन्नाद्येन तेजसा ब्रह्मवर्चसेन ॥ ५.२३.२ ॥ ॥ इति त्रयोविंशः खण्डः ॥

*udāne tṛpyati tvaktṛpyati tvaci tṛpyantyāṃ vāyustṛpyati vāyau tṛpyatyākāśastṛpyatyākāśe tṛpyati yatkiṃca vāyuścākāśaścādhitiṣṭhatastattṛpyati tasyānu tṛptiṃ tṛpyati prajayā paśubhirannādyena tejasā brahmavarcasena || 5.23.2 || || iti trayoviṃśaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||*

If udāna is pleased, the organ of touch is pleased. If the organ of touch is pleased, air is pleased. If air is pleased, space is pleased. If space is pleased, everything governed by air and space is pleased.
